    else if (c === "\n") { row.push(field); field = ""; rows.push(row); row = []; }
    else field += c;
  }
  if (field !== "" || row.length) { row.push(field); rows.push(row); }
  return rows;
}

// Chuan hoa tieu de cot de tu map: bo dau, viet thuong, bo ky tu khac chu/so.
function normGsheetHeader(s) {
  return String(s == null ? "" : s).normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").replace(/đ/g, "d").replace(/Đ/g, "D").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
}

// Goi y map cot: tieu de sheet (da chuan hoa) khop voi ten cot DB hoac nhan
// cot. Tra { "0": "so_thue_bao", ... } — key la index cot trong vung (0-based).
function suggestGsheetMap(headers, columns) {
  const byKey = new Map(), byLabel = new Map();
  for (const [key, , label] of columns) {
    const nk = normGsheetHeader(key);
    if (!byKey.has(nk)) byKey.set(nk, key);
    const nl = normGsheetHeader(label);
    if (nl && !byLabel.has(nl)) byLabel.set(nl, key);
  }
  const used = new Set(), map = {};
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    const nh = normGsheetHeader(h);
    if (!nh) return;
    const hit = byKey.get(nh) || byLabel.get(nh);
    if (hit && !used.has(hit)) { used.add(hit); map[String(i)] = hit; }
  });
  return map;
}

// Kiem tra dinh dang vung lay du lieu (khong can biet kich thuoc sheet):
// tra cac chi so 0-based (null = lay tu dau/den het). Nem loi khi sai.
function parseGsheetRange(cfg) {
  const sc = colRefToIndex(cfg.start_col);
  const ec = colRefToIndex(cfg.end_col);
  const hasSr = cfg.start_row != null && cfg.start_row !== "";
  const hasEr = cfg.end_row != null && cfg.end_row !== "";
  const sr = hasSr ? Number(cfg.start_row) - 1 : null;
  const er = hasEr ? Number(cfg.end_row) - 1 : null;
  if (cfg.start_col != null && cfg.start_col !== "" && sc === null) throw new Error("Cot bat dau khong hop le (nhap chu cot VD: A, hoac so VD: 1)");
  if (cfg.end_col != null && cfg.end_col !== "" && ec === null) throw new Error("Cot ket thuc khong hop le (nhap chu cot VD: Z, hoac so VD: 26)");
  if (hasSr && (!Number.isInteger(Number(cfg.start_row)) || sr < 0)) throw new Error("Dong bat dau khong hop le (phai la so nguyen >= 1)");
  if (hasEr && (!Number.isInteger(Number(cfg.end_row)) || er < 0)) throw new Error("Dong ket thuc khong hop le (phai la so nguyen >= 1)");
  if (sr != null && er != null && sr > er) throw new Error("Dong bat dau lon hon dong ket thuc");
  if (sc != null && ec != null && sc > ec) throw new Error("Cot bat dau lon hon cot ket thuc");
  return { sc, sr, ec, er };
}

// Ap vung lay du lieu len luoi CSV: dong dau cua vung la tieu de.
// Tra {headers, rows, totalRows, totalCols}. End vuot qua sheet thi kep lai.
function applyGsheetRange(grid, cfg) {
  const totalRows = grid.length;
  const totalCols = grid.reduce((m, r) => Math.max(m, r.length), 0);
  if (totalRows === 0) throw new Error("Sheet khong co du lieu");
  const { sc, sr, ec, er } = parseGsheetRange(cfg);
  const c0 = sc == null ? 0 : sc;
  const c1 = ec == null ? totalCols - 1 : Math.min(ec, totalCols - 1);
  const r0 = sr == null ? 0 : sr;
  const r1 = er == null ? totalRows - 1 : Math.min(er, totalRows - 1);
  if (r0 >= totalRows) throw new Error("Dong bat dau (" + (r0 + 1) + ") vuot qua so dong cua sheet (" + totalRows + " dong)");
  if (c0 >= totalCols) throw new Error("Cot bat dau vuot qua so cot cua sheet (" + totalCols + " cot)");
  if (r0 > r1) throw new Error("Dong bat dau lon hon dong ket thuc (sau khi kep theo kich thuoc sheet)");
  if (c0 > c1) throw new Error("Cot bat dau lon hon cot ket thuc (sau khi kep theo kich thuoc sheet)");
  const slice = (r) => {
    const out = [];
    for (let c = c0; c <= c1; c++) out.push(r[c] == null ? "" : String(r[c]));
    return out;
  };
  const headers = slice(grid[r0]);
  const rows = [];
  for (let r = r0 + 1; r <= r1; r++) rows.push(slice(grid[r]));
  return { headers, rows, totalRows: rows.length, totalCols: c1 - c0 + 1 };
}

// Tai sheet cong khai ve dang luoi: thu export CSV truoc, neu that bai thi
// fallback gviz JSON (khong can Publish to web, chi can link cong khai).
// Loi tra ve thong bao ro rang cho admin kiem tra quyen chia se.
async function fetchSheetGrid(sheetUrl, gidOverride) {
  const { id, gid } = parseSheetLink(sheetUrl);
  const g = gidOverride != null && String(gidOverride) !== "" ? String(gidOverride) : gid;
  const csvUrl = "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/" + id + "/export?format=csv&gid=" + encodeURIComponent(g);
  let csvErr = null;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(csvUrl, { redirect: "follow" });
    const text = await res.text();
    if (res.ok && text && !text.trimStart().startsWith("<")) return parseGsheetCsv(text);
    csvErr = "HTTP " + res.status;
  } catch (e) { csvErr = e && e.message ? e.message : String(e); }
  try {
    const res = await fetch("https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/" + id + "/gviz/tq?tqx=out:json&gid=" + encodeURIComponent(g), { redirect: "follow" });
    const text = await res.text();
    const m = text.match(/google\.visualization\.Query\.setResponse\(([\s\S]*)\)\s*;?\s*$/);
    if (res.ok && m) {
      const j = JSON.parse(m[1]);
      if (j && j.status === "ok" && j.table && Array.isArray(j.table.rows)) {
        const ncols = (j.table.cols || []).length;
        return j.table.rows.map((r) => {
          const cells = (r && r.c) || [];
          const out = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < ncols; i++) {
            const cell = cells[i];
            out.push(cell == null ? "" : (cell.v != null ? String(cell.v) : (cell.f != null ? String(cell.f) : "")));
          }
          return out;
        });
      }
    }
  } catch (e2) { /* roi xuong loi chung ben duoi */ }
  throw new Error("Khong tai duoc Google Sheet (" + (csvErr || "khong ro nguyen nhan") + "). Hay kiem tra: link dung va sheet da chia se o che do 'Bat ky ai co duong lien ket deu co the xem'.");
}

async function uploadPackageImage(env, pkgId, file) {
  if (!env.ANH) throw new Error("Chua cau hinh R2 Object Storage (chua gan bucket ANH cho Worker). Xem huong dan cau hinh R2 trong module Danh muc goi cuoc.");
  const mime = file.type || "";
  if (!mime.startsWith("image/")) throw new Error("File anh phai la dinh dang anh (jpg, png, webp, gif)");
  if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error("Anh vuot qua 5MB. Hay nen anh nho hon roi thu lai.");
  const ext = mime === "image/jpeg" ? "jpg" : mime === "image/png" ? "png" : mime === "image/webp" ? "webp" : mime === "image/gif" ? "gif" : "";
  if (!ext) throw new Error("Dinh dang anh khong duoc ho tro (chi nhan jpg, png, webp, gif)");
  const key = "goi-cuoc/" + pkgId + "-" + Date.now() + "." + ext;
  await env.ANH.put(key, await file.arrayBuffer(), { httpMetadata: { contentType: mime } });
  return key;
}
async function readPackageForm(request) {
  const ct = request.headers.get("content-type") || "";
  if (ct.includes("multipart/form-data")) {
    const fd = await request.formData();
    const file = fd.get("anh");
    return {
      ten_goi: toText(fd.get("ten_goi")),
      chu_ky: toText(fd.get("chu_ky")),
      gia_goi: Number(fd.get("gia_goi")) || 0,
      uu_dai: toText(fd.get("uu_dai")),
      file: file && file.size > 0 ? file : null,
    };
  }
  const b = await readBody(request);
  return { ten_goi: toText(b.ten_goi), chu_ky: toText(b.chu_ky), gia_goi: Number(b.gia_goi) || 0, uu_dai: toText(b.uu_dai), file: null };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Xu ly chinh
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const path = url.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, "") || "/";
    const method = request.method;

    if (method === "OPTIONS") {
      return new Response(null, {
        headers: {
          "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
          "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS",
          "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, Authorization",
        },
      });
    }
    if (!env.DB) return err("Worker chua duoc gan (binding) voi CSDL D1. Hay kiem tra lai buoc Binding trong huong dan.", 500);

    try {
      // ---- Kiem tra ket noi (cong khai, dung cho nut "Test ket noi") ----
      if (path === "/api/health" && method === "GET") {
        await env.DB.prepare("SELECT 1").first();
        return json({ ok: true, db: true, message: "Ket noi CSDL D1 thanh cong" });
      }

      // ---- Dang nhap ----
      if (path === "/api/login" && method === "POST") {
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const username = toText(b.username);
        const password = String(b.password || "");
        if (!username || !password) return err("Vui long nhap ten dang nhap va mat khau", 422);
        // Tim tai khoan theo username truoc; khong co thi thu theo so Eload
        // (diem ban dang nhap bang so Eload, nhu huong dan tren o dang nhap).
        let user = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM nguoi_dung WHERE username = ?")
          .bind(username)
          .first();
        if (!user) {
          user = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM nguoi_dung WHERE so_eload = ? AND so_eload IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(so_eload) != ''")
            .bind(username)
            .first();
        }
        if (!user || !user.active) return err("Tai khoan khong ton tai hoac da bi khoa", 401);
        const hash = await hashPassword(password, user.salt);
        if (hash !== user.password_hash) return err("Mat khau khong dung", 401);
        await withUnitName(env, user);
        // Ghi log dang nhap (phuc vu module Thong ke dang nhap)
        try {
          await ensureLoginLog(env);
          await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO dang_nhap_log (user_id, username, ho_ten, thoi_gian) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)")
            .bind(user.id, user.username, user.ho_ten, saigonNow())
            .run();
        } catch (e) { /* loi ghi log khong duoc chan dang nhap */ }
        // Tai khoan van dung mat khau mac dinh -> bat doi mat khau truoc khi
        // vao he thong (chi cap token doi mat khau 15 phut, khong vao duoc
        // cac chuc nang khac).
        if (password === DEFAULT_PASSWORD) {
          const token = await signToken(env, {
            sub: user.id,
            username: user.username,
            role: user.role,
            pwchange: 1,
            exp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + PWCHANGE_TTL_SECONDS,
          });
          return json({ ok: true, must_change_password: true, token, user: publicUser(user) });
        }
        const token = await signToken(env, {
          sub: user.id,
          username: user.username,
          role: user.role,
          exp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS,
        });
        return json({ ok: true, token, user: publicUser(user) });
      }

      // ---- Doi mat khau (tu doi sau khi dang nhap, hoac bat buoc doi o lan
      // dau khi tai khoan van dung mat khau mac dinh). Chap nhan ca token
      // doi mat khau (pwchange) va token dang nhap thuong. Mat khau moi phai
      // >= 8 ky tu va khac mat khau mac dinh. ----
      if (path === "/api/change-password" && method === "POST") {
        const h = request.headers.get("Authorization") || "";
        const m = h.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i);
        if (!m) return err("Phien dang nhap khong hop le hoac da het han. Vui long dang nhap lai.", 401);
        const payload = await verifyToken(env, m[1]);
        if (!payload) return err("Phien dang nhap khong hop le hoac da het han. Vui long dang nhap lai.", 401);
        const user = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ?")
          .bind(payload.sub)
          .first();
        if (!user || !user.active) return err("Tai khoan khong ton tai hoac da bi khoa", 401);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const np = String(b.new_password || "");
        if (np.length < 8) return err("Mat khau moi phai co it nhat 8 ky tu", 422);
        if (np === DEFAULT_PASSWORD) return err("Mat khau moi khong duoc trung voi mat khau mac dinh", 422);
        const salt = randomSalt();
        const hash = await hashPassword(np, salt);
        await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE nguoi_dung SET password_hash = ?, salt = ? WHERE id = ?")
          .bind(hash, salt, user.id)
          .run();
        const token = await signToken(env, {
          sub: user.id,
          username: user.username,
          role: user.role,
          exp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS,
        });
        return json({ ok: true, token, user: publicUser(await withUnitName(env, user)) });
      }

      // ---- Danh muc goi cuoc: cong khai, KHONG can dang nhap ----
      // (cong cu Uoc tinh tren trang chu cong khai cung dung danh muc nay)
      if (path === "/api/packages" && method === "GET") {
        await ensurePackages(env);
        const res = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM goi_cuoc ORDER BY ten_goi, chu_ky").all();
        return json({ ok: true, packages: res.results || [] });
      }

      // ---- Anh truyen thong goi cuoc: phuc vu qua Worker, KHONG can dang nhap
      // (the <img> khong gui duoc token Authorization, nen endpoint nay phai
      //  dung truoc khoi yeu cau dang nhap phia duoi)
      const imgMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/packages\/image\/(.+)$/);
      if (imgMatch && method === "GET") {
        const key = decodeURIComponent(imgMatch[1]);
        if (!/^goi-cuoc\/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$/.test(key)) return err("Anh khong hop le", 400);
        if (!env.ANH) return err("Chua cau hinh R2 Object Storage", 501);
        const obj = await env.ANH.get(key);
        if (!obj) return err("Khong tim thay anh", 404);
        const headers = new Headers();
        obj.writeHttpMetadata(headers);
        headers.set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=86400");
        headers.set("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");
        return new Response(obj.body, { headers });
      }

      // ---- Cac API con lai deu can dang nhap ----
      await ensureUserColumns(env); // dam bao cac cot bo sung da co truoc khi truy van
      const me = await requireAuth(request, env);
      if (!me) return err("Phien dang nhap khong hop le hoac da het han. Vui long dang nhap lai.", 401);
      const isAdmin = me.role === "admin";
      const isSubAdmin = me.role === "subadmin";
      // SubAdmin dung day du quyen nhu Admin (17/11), TRU: khoa/mo khoa bang,
      // xoa bang du lieu, thay the bang dang khoa, cap role admin/subadmin va
      // xem module Cau hinh ket noi D1 — cac cho do van giu isAdmin.
      const isAdminLike = isAdmin || isSubAdmin;
      const isLeader = me.role === "leader";
      // Chuyen vien (nhan su noi bo role staff + chuc danh "Chuyên viên"):
      // duoc xem so lieu tuong duong Lanh dao don vi — pham vi cay don vi
      // cua minh. Khong ap dung cho module Cham soc diem ban (van chi
      // admin/lanh dao duoc gan diem truc tiep).
      const isLeaderLike = isLeader || (me.role === "staff" && me.chuc_danh === "Chuyên viên");
      // Lanh dao don vi: xem du lieu cua cac user trong don vi cua minh VA
      // cac don vi truc thuoc (cay don_vi qua parent_id); neu chua gan don
      // vi thi tam xem du lieu cua chinh minh.
      const isUnitScoped = isLeaderLike && !!me.don_vi_id;
      const isPrivileged = isAdmin || isSubAdmin; // Admin & SubAdmin: xem du lieu toan tinh
      const canManageUsers = isAdmin || isSubAdmin; // Admin & SubAdmin: quan ly nguoi dung

      if (path === "/api/me" && method === "GET") {
        return json({ ok: true, user: publicUser(await withUnitName(env, me)) });
      }

      // ---- Quan ly don vi ----
      if (path === "/api/units" && method === "GET") {
        const res = await env.DB.prepare(
          "SELECT id, ma_don_vi, ten_don_vi, parent_id FROM don_vi ORDER BY ma_don_vi"
        ).all();
        return json({ ok: true, units: res.results || [] });
      }
      if (path === "/api/units" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "units")) return err("Chi admin/subadmin hoac tai khoan duoc cap module Quan ly don vi moi co quyen tao don vi", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        if (!toText(b.ma_don_vi) || !toText(b.ten_don_vi)) return err("Thieu ma don vi hoac ten don vi", 422);
        const r = await env.DB.prepare(
          "INSERT INTO don_vi (ma_don_vi, ten_don_vi, parent_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)"
        )
          .bind(toText(b.ma_don_vi), toText(b.ten_don_vi), b.parent_id ? Number(b.parent_id) : null)
          .run();
        return json({ ok: true, id: r.meta.last_row_id });
      }
      const unitMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/units\/(\d+)$/);
      if (unitMatch && method === "PUT") {
        if (!isAdminLike && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "units")) return err("Chi admin/subadmin hoac tai khoan duoc cap module Quan ly don vi moi co quyen sua don vi", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE don_vi SET ma_don_vi = ?, ten_don_vi = ?, parent_id = ? WHERE id = ?")
          .bind(toText(b.ma_don_vi), toText(b.ten_don_vi), b.parent_id ? Number(b.parent_id) : null, Number(unitMatch[1]))
          .run();
        return json({ ok: true });
      }
      if (unitMatch && method === "DELETE") {
        if (!isAdminLike && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "units")) return err("Chi admin/subadmin hoac tai khoan duoc cap module Quan ly don vi moi co quyen xoa don vi", 403);
        await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE don_vi SET parent_id = NULL WHERE parent_id = ?").bind(Number(unitMatch[1])).run();
        await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM don_vi WHERE id = ?").bind(Number(unitMatch[1])).run();
        return json({ ok: true });
      }

      // ---- Quan ly kenh ban (module "Quan ly kenh ban", key users) va
      // Quan ly nguoi dung noi bo (module "Quan ly nguoi dung", key staff).
      // kind=channel: chi tai khoan diem ban (role='user');
      // kind=staff: tai khoan noi bo (admin/subadmin/leader/staff).
      if (path === "/api/users" && method === "GET") {
        if (!canManageUsers && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "users") && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "staff")) return err("Chi admin/subadmin hoac tai khoan duoc cap module Quan ly kenh ban / Quan ly nguoi dung moi co quyen xem danh sach", 403);
        const kind = url.searchParams.get("kind");
        const needKey = kind === "staff" ? "staff" : kind === "channel" ? "users" : null;
        if (needKey ? !hasModule(me, needKey) : !(hasModule(me, "users") || hasModule(me, "staff")))
          return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module nay", 403);
        const where = kind === "channel" ? " WHERE u.role = 'user'" : kind === "staff" ? " WHERE u.role != 'user'" : "";
        const res = await env.DB.prepare(
          "SELECT u.id, u.ma_kenh, u.ho_ten, u.username, u.role, u.so_eload, u.so_vi, u.ten_nhan_vien, u.sdt_nhan_vien, u.ma_hrm, u.ma_nhan_vien, u.chuc_danh, u.don_vi_id, d.ten_don_vi AS don_vi_ten, u.leader_id, l.ho_ten AS leader_ten, u.loai_kenh, u.mien_cham_soc, u.can_import, u.modules, u.lat, u.lng, u.active FROM nguoi_dung u LEFT JOIN don_vi d ON d.id = u.don_vi_id LEFT JOIN nguoi_dung l ON l.id = u.leader_id" + where + " ORDER BY u.ho_ten"
        ).all();
        return json({ ok: true, users: (res.results || []).map(publicUser) });
      }
      // Danh sach chuc danh trong module Quan ly nguoi dung (nhan su noi bo)
      const CHUC_DANH_LIST = ["Lãnh đạo đơn vị", "Chuyên viên", "Nhân viên kinh doanh", "Quản trị viên"];
      // Chuc danh + co SubAdmin -> role he thong
      const staffRoleFrom = (chucDanh, isSubAdmin) => {
        if (isSubAdmin) return "subadmin";
        const cd = String(chucDanh || "").trim();
        if (cd === "Quản trị viên") return "admin";
        if (cd === "Lãnh đạo đơn vị") return "leader";
        return "staff"; // Chuyên viên / Nhân viên kinh doanh: chi xem so lieu cua chinh minh
      };
      // Module mac dinh khi tao tai khoan nhan su (admin khong tich tay)
      const defaultModulesForRole = (role) => {
        if (role === "admin") return null;
        if (role === "leader") return JSON.stringify(LEADER_MODULES);
        return JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_MODULES);
      };
      if (path === "/api/users" && method === "POST") {
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const kind = b.kind === "staff" ? "staff" : "channel";
        const needKey = kind === "staff" ? "staff" : "users";
        if (!canManageUsers && !hasModuleExplicit(me, needKey)) return err("Chi admin/subadmin hoac tai khoan duoc cap module nay moi co quyen tao nguoi dung", 403);
        if (!hasModule(me, needKey)) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module nay", 403);
        if (!toText(b.ho_ten) || !toText(b.username)) return err("Thieu ho ten hoac user dang nhap", 422);
        const salt = randomSalt();
        const password = String(b.password || "Vnpt@2026");
        const hash = await hashPassword(password, salt);
        let newRole, newCanImport, newModules, newMaNv = "", newChucDanh = "";
        if (kind === "channel") {
          // Kenh ban: luon la user, khong co quyen import; mac dinh 4 module
          // (Dashboard, Bao cao thong ke, Danh muc goi cuoc, Cai dat).
          newRole = "user";
          newCanImport = 0;
          if (isAdminLike && Array.isArray(b.modules)) newModules = JSON.stringify(b.modules.filter((k) => MODULE_KEYS.includes(k)));
          else newModules = JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_MODULES);
        } else {
          newMaNv = toText(b.ma_nhan_vien);
          newChucDanh = toText(b.chuc_danh);
          if (newChucDanh && !CHUC_DANH_LIST.includes(newChucDanh)) return err("Chuc danh khong hop le", 422);
          if (isAdmin) {
            newRole = staffRoleFrom(newChucDanh, b.subadmin);
          } else {
            // SubAdmin / user co quyen Quan ly nguoi dung tao nhan su: khong duoc cap subadmin/admin
            newRole = newChucDanh === "Lãnh đạo đơn vị" ? "leader" : "staff";
          }
          // Quyen import: chi admin/subadmin duoc cap khi tao (user co tick
          // module Quan ly nguoi dung thi khong — tranh tu cap quyen).
          newCanImport = isAdminLike && b.can_import ? 1 : 0;
          if (isAdminLike && Array.isArray(b.modules)) newModules = JSON.stringify(b.modules.filter((k) => MODULE_KEYS.includes(k)));
          else newModules = defaultModulesForRole(newRole);
        }
        const latP = parseCoordField(b.lat, -90, 90);
        if (latP.error) return err("Vi do diem ban khong hop le (tu -90 den 90)", 422);
        const lngP = parseCoordField(b.lng, -180, 180);
        if (lngP.error) return err("Kinh do diem ban khong hop le (tu -180 den 180)", 422);
        // Lanh dao phu trach: chi nhan id cua tai khoan co role leader.
        let newLeaderId = null;
        if (b.leader_id) {
          const ld = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ? AND role = 'leader'").bind(Number(b.leader_id)).first();
          if (!ld) return err("Lanh dao phu trach khong ton tai hoac khong phai tai khoan Lanh dao don vi", 422);
          newLeaderId = ld.id;
        }
        // Loai kenh ban: DUQ/DBL/GARA/DDV (hoac de trong).
        const newLoaiKenh = normLoaiKenh(b.loai_kenh);
        if (toText(b.loai_kenh) && !newLoaiKenh) return err("Loai kenh ban khong hop le (DUQ/DBL/GARA/DDV)", 422);
        const r = await env.DB.prepare(
          "INSERT INTO nguoi_dung (ma_kenh, ho_ten, username, password_hash, salt, role, so_eload, so_vi, ten_nhan_vien, sdt_nhan_vien, ma_hrm, ma_nhan_vien, chuc_danh, don_vi_id, leader_id, loai_kenh, mien_cham_soc, can_import, modules, lat, lng) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
        )
          .bind(
            toText(b.ma_kenh), toText(b.ho_ten), toText(b.username), hash, salt,
            newRole,
            toText(b.so_eload), toText(b.so_vi), toText(b.ten_nhan_vien), toText(b.sdt_nhan_vien), toText(b.ma_hrm),
            newMaNv, newChucDanh,
            b.don_vi_id ? Number(b.don_vi_id) : null, newLeaderId, newLoaiKenh, kind === "channel" && b.mien_cham_soc ? 1 : 0, newCanImport, newModules, latP.val, lngP.val
          )
          .run();
        return json({ ok: true, id: r.meta.last_row_id });
      }
      // ---- Import nguoi dung tu Excel (kind=channel: diem ban; kind=staff: nhan su noi bo) ----
      if (path === "/api/users/import" && method === "POST") {
        if (!canManageUsers) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi co quyen import", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const kind = b.kind === "staff" ? "staff" : "channel";
        const needKey = kind === "staff" ? "staff" : "users";
        if (!hasModule(me, needKey)) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module nay", 403);
        const rows = Array.isArray(b.rows) ? b.rows : [];
        if (!rows.length) return err("Khong co dong du lieu nao de import", 422);
        // Che do import: create (mac dinh) | update | replace
        // - create: chi them user chua co; user da ton tai thi bo qua.
        // - update: user da co thi cap nhat cac truong co du lieu moi trong file
        //   (truong de trong thi giu nguyen); chua co thi tao moi.
        // - replace: user da co thi ghi de toan bo thong tin theo file
        //   (truong de trong thi xoa); chua co thi tao moi.
        // Import khong bao gio doi role / can_import / modules cua user da co
        // (doi cac quyen nay trong form Sua); mat khau de trong thi giu nguyen
        // mat khau cu (tao moi thi dung mac dinh Vnpt@2026).
        const impMode = b.mode === "update" ? "update" : b.mode === "replace" ? "replace" : "create";
        let created = 0, updated = 0, skipped = 0;
        const errors = [];
        const warnings = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
          const r = rows[i] || {};
          const username = toText(r.username);
          const hoTen = toText(r.ho_ten);
          if (!username || !hoTen) {
            skipped++;
            errors.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "Thieu User dang nhap hoac Ho va ten" });
            continue;
          }
          const existing = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM nguoi_dung WHERE username = ?").bind(username).first();
          // Don vi: nhan id truc tiep, hoac tra theo ma/ten don vi trong file Excel
          let donViId = null, donViOk = false;
          if (r.don_vi_id) { donViId = Number(r.don_vi_id) || null; donViOk = true; }
          else if (toText(r.don_vi)) {
            const dv = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM don_vi WHERE ma_don_vi = ? OR ten_don_vi = ?")
              .bind(toText(r.don_vi), toText(r.don_vi))
              .first();
            if (dv) { donViId = dv.id; donViOk = true; }
            else warnings.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "Khong tim thay don vi '" + toText(r.don_vi) + "' — da de trong" });
          }
          // Toa do / Lanh dao phu trach / Loai kenh ban: chi ap dung cho diem ban (kind=channel)
          let impLat = null, impLng = null, latOk = false, lngOk = false;
          let leaderId = null, leaderOk = false;
          let impLoaiKenh = "";
          if (kind === "channel") {
            if (toText(r.lat) !== "") {
              const p = parseCoordField(r.lat, -90, 90);
              if (p.error) warnings.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "Vi do khong hop le — da giu nguyen" });
              else { impLat = p.val; latOk = true; }
            }
            if (toText(r.lng) !== "") {
              const p = parseCoordField(r.lng, -180, 180);
              if (p.error) warnings.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "Kinh do khong hop le — da giu nguyen" });
              else { impLng = p.val; lngOk = true; }
            }
            if (toText(r.leader_username)) {
              const ld = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM nguoi_dung WHERE username = ? AND role = 'leader'").bind(toText(r.leader_username)).first();
              if (ld) { leaderId = ld.id; leaderOk = true; }
              else warnings.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "Khong tim thay tai khoan lanh dao '" + toText(r.leader_username) + "' — da de trong" });
            }
            impLoaiKenh = normLoaiKenh(r.loai_kenh);
            if (toText(r.loai_kenh) && !impLoaiKenh) warnings.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "Loai kenh ban '" + toText(r.loai_kenh) + "' khong nhan ra — da de trong" });
          }
          // Truong nhan su: chi ap dung cho kind=staff
          let impMaNv = "", impChucDanh = "Nhân viên kinh doanh", impSubAdmin = false, impCanImport = 0, impModules = null;
          if (kind === "staff") {
            impMaNv = toText(r.ma_nhan_vien);
            const cdRaw = toText(r.chuc_danh);
            if (cdRaw && !CHUC_DANH_LIST.includes(cdRaw)) warnings.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "Chuc danh '" + cdRaw + "' khong hop le — da dung 'Nhân viên kinh doanh'" });
            else if (cdRaw) impChucDanh = cdRaw;
            impSubAdmin = !!r.subadmin;
            impCanImport = isAdminLike && r.can_import ? 1 : 0;
            if (isAdminLike && toText(r.modules)) {
              const keys = String(r.modules).split(",").map((s) => s.trim().toLowerCase()).filter((k) => MODULE_KEYS.includes(k));
              if (keys.length) impModules = JSON.stringify(keys);
              else warnings.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "Cot Modules khong co ma hop le — da dung mac dinh theo chuc danh" });
            }
          }
          if (existing) {
            if (impMode === "create") {
              skipped++;
              errors.push({ row: i + 2, reason: "User dang nhap da ton tai: " + username });
              continue;
            }
            const sets = [], binds = [];
            const setText = (col, raw) => {
              const v = toText(raw);
              if (impMode === "update") { if (v) { sets.push(col + " = ?"); binds.push(v); } }
              else { sets.push(col + " = ?"); binds.push(v); }
            };
            if (kind === "staff") {
              // Nhan su: chi cap nhat thong tin hanh chinh; khong doi role/can_import/modules
              setText("ma_nhan_vien", r.ma_nhan_vien);
              setText("ho_ten", r.ho_ten);
              if (impMode === "replace") { sets.push("chuc_danh = ?"); binds.push(impChucDanh); }
              else if (toText(r.chuc_danh)) { sets.push("chuc_danh = ?"); binds.push(impChucDanh); }
              if (impMode === "replace") { sets.push("don_vi_id = ?"); binds.push(donViOk ? donViId : null); }
              else if (donViOk) { sets.push("don_vi_id = ?"); binds.push(donViId); }
            } else {
              setText("ma_kenh", r.ma_kenh);
              setText("ho_ten", r.ho_ten);
              setText("so_eload", r.so_eload);
              setText("so_vi", r.so_vi);
              setText("ten_nhan_vien", r.ten_nhan_vien);
              setText("sdt_nhan_vien", r.sdt_nhan_vien);
              setText("ma_hrm", r.ma_hrm);
              if (impMode === "replace") { sets.push("loai_kenh = ?"); binds.push(impLoaiKenh); }
              else if (impLoaiKenh) { sets.push("loai_kenh = ?"); binds.push(impLoaiKenh); }
              if (impMode === "replace") { sets.push("don_vi_id = ?"); binds.push(donViOk ? donViId : null); }
              else if (donViOk) { sets.push("don_vi_id = ?"); binds.push(donViId); }
              if (impMode === "replace") { sets.push("leader_id = ?"); binds.push(leaderOk ? leaderId : null); }
              else if (leaderOk) { sets.push("leader_id = ?"); binds.push(leaderId); }
              if (impMode === "replace") { sets.push("lat = ?"); binds.push(latOk ? impLat : null); sets.push("lng = ?"); binds.push(lngOk ? impLng : null); }
              else { if (latOk) { sets.push("lat = ?"); binds.push(impLat); } if (lngOk) { sets.push("lng = ?"); binds.push(impLng); } }
            }
            const pw = String(r.password || "");
            let pwHash = null, pwSalt = null;
            if (pw) { pwSalt = randomSalt(); pwHash = await hashPassword(pw, pwSalt); }
            if (sets.length || pwHash) {
              let sql = "UPDATE nguoi_dung SET " + sets.join(", ");
              if (pwHash) sql += (sets.length ? ", " : "") + "password_hash = ?, salt = ?";
              sql += " WHERE id = ?";
              const allBinds = binds.concat(pwHash ? [pwHash, pwSalt] : []).concat([existing.id]);
              await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...allBinds).run();
            }
            updated++;
            continue;
          }
          const salt = randomSalt();
          const hash = await hashPassword(String(r.password || "Vnpt@2026"), salt);
          if (kind === "staff") {
            // Nhan su moi: role suy tu chuc danh + SubAdmin (subadmin import thi khong duoc cap subadmin/admin)
            const newRole = isAdmin ? staffRoleFrom(impChucDanh, impSubAdmin) : (impChucDanh === "Lãnh đạo đơn vị" ? "leader" : "staff");
            const newModules = impModules || defaultModulesForRole(newRole);
            await env.DB.prepare(
              "INSERT INTO nguoi_dung (ho_ten, username, password_hash, salt, role, ma_nhan_vien, chuc_danh, don_vi_id, can_import, modules) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
            )
              .bind(hoTen, username, hash, salt, newRole, impMaNv, impChucDanh, donViOk ? donViId : null, impCanImport, newModules)
              .run();
          } else {
            // Diem ban moi: luon la user, khong co quyen import, mac dinh 4 module
            await env.DB.prepare(
              "INSERT INTO nguoi_dung (ma_kenh, ho_ten, username, password_hash, salt, role, so_eload, so_vi, ten_nhan_vien, sdt_nhan_vien, ma_hrm, don_vi_id, leader_id, loai_kenh, can_import, modules, lat, lng) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
            )
              .bind(
                toText(r.ma_kenh), hoTen, username, hash, salt,
                "user",
                toText(r.so_eload), toText(r.so_vi), toText(r.ten_nhan_vien), toText(r.sdt_nhan_vien), toText(r.ma_hrm), donViOk ? donViId : null, leaderOk ? leaderId : null, impLoaiKenh, 0,
                JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_MODULES),
                latOk ? impLat : null, lngOk ? impLng : null
              )
              .run();
          }
          created++;
        }
        return json({ ok: true, created, updated, skipped, errors: errors.slice(0, 50), warnings: warnings.slice(0, 50) });
      }
      const userMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/users\/(\d+)$/);
      if (userMatch && method === "PUT") {
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const kind = b.kind === "staff" ? "staff" : "channel";
        const needKey = kind === "staff" ? "staff" : "users";
        if (!canManageUsers && !hasModuleExplicit(me, needKey)) return err("Chi admin/subadmin hoac tai khoan duoc cap module nay moi co quyen sua nguoi dung", 403);
        if (!hasModule(me, needKey)) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module nay", 403);
        const uid = Number(userMatch[1]);
        const cur = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT role, can_import, modules, chuc_danh FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ?").bind(uid).first();
        if (!cur) return err("Khong tim thay nguoi dung", 404);
        // Khong cho sua tai khoan khac module (kenh ban <-> nhan su)
        if (kind === "channel" && cur.role !== "user") return err("Tai khoan nay thuoc module Quan ly nguoi dung", 422);
        if (kind === "staff" && cur.role === "user") return err("Tai khoan nay thuoc module Quan ly kenh ban", 422);
        if (!toText(b.ho_ten)) return err("Thieu ho ten", 422);
        let newRole = cur.role, newCanImport = cur.can_import, newModules = cur.modules;
        let newMaNv = null, newChucDanh = null; // null = khong doi cot nay
        if (kind === "staff") {
          newMaNv = toText(b.ma_nhan_vien);
          newChucDanh = ("chuc_danh" in b) ? toText(b.chuc_danh) : (cur.chuc_danh || "");
          if (newChucDanh && !CHUC_DANH_LIST.includes(newChucDanh)) return err("Chuc danh khong hop le", 422);
          const subAdm = ("subadmin" in b) ? !!b.subadmin : cur.role === "subadmin";
          // Doi role: CHI admin (cap/nang/hạ admin/subadmin la quyen rieng cua admin).
          if (isAdmin) {
            newRole = staffRoleFrom(newChucDanh, subAdm);
          } else {
            // SubAdmin / user co quyen Quan ly nguoi dung: khong duoc nang/hạ
            // loai tai khoan admin/subadmin; chi doi duoc leader <-> staff.
            if (cur.role === "admin" || cur.role === "subadmin") newRole = cur.role;
            else newRole = newChucDanh === "Lãnh đạo đơn vị" ? "leader" : "staff";
          }
          // Quyen import + modules: admin/subadmin duoc dat; user chi co tick
          // module Quan ly nguoi dung thi KHONG (tranh tu cap quyen cho minh).
          if (isAdminLike) {
            newCanImport = b.can_import ? 1 : 0;
            if (Array.isArray(b.modules)) newModules = JSON.stringify(b.modules.filter((k) => MODULE_KEYS.includes(k)));
          }
          if (uid === me.id && newRole !== me.role) return err("Khong the tu doi loai tai khoan cua chinh minh", 422);
        } else {
          newRole = "user";
          if (isAdminLike && Array.isArray(b.modules)) newModules = JSON.stringify(b.modules.filter((k) => MODULE_KEYS.includes(k)));
        }
        const latP = parseCoordField(b.lat, -90, 90);
        if (latP.error) return err("Vi do diem ban khong hop le (tu -90 den 90)", 422);
        const lngP = parseCoordField(b.lng, -180, 180);
        if (lngP.error) return err("Kinh do diem ban khong hop le (tu -180 den 180)", 422);
        let newLeaderId = null;
        if (b.leader_id) {
          const ld = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ? AND role = 'leader'").bind(Number(b.leader_id)).first();
          if (!ld) return err("Lanh dao phu trach khong ton tai hoac khong phai tai khoan Lanh dao don vi", 422);
          newLeaderId = ld.id;
        }
        const newLoaiKenh = normLoaiKenh(b.loai_kenh);
        if (toText(b.loai_kenh) && !newLoaiKenh) return err("Loai kenh ban khong hop le (DUQ/DBL/GARA/DDV)", 422);
        const sets = ["ho_ten = ?", "don_vi_id = ?", "active = ?", "role = ?", "can_import = ?", "modules = ?"];
        const binds = [toText(b.ho_ten), b.don_vi_id ? Number(b.don_vi_id) : null, b.active === false ? 0 : 1, newRole, newCanImport, newModules];
        if (kind === "staff") {
          sets.push("ma_nhan_vien = ?", "chuc_danh = ?"); binds.push(newMaNv, newChucDanh);
        } else {
          sets.push("ma_kenh = ?", "so_eload = ?", "so_vi = ?", "ten_nhan_vien = ?", "sdt_nhan_vien = ?", "ma_hrm = ?",
            "leader_id = ?", "loai_kenh = ?", "mien_cham_soc = ?", "lat = ?", "lng = ?");
          binds.push(toText(b.ma_kenh), toText(b.so_eload), toText(b.so_vi), toText(b.ten_nhan_vien),
            toText(b.sdt_nhan_vien), toText(b.ma_hrm), newLeaderId, newLoaiKenh, b.mien_cham_soc ? 1 : 0, latP.val, lngP.val);
        }
        await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE nguoi_dung SET " + sets.join(", ") + " WHERE id = ?").bind(...binds, uid).run();
        if (b.password) {
          const salt = randomSalt();
          const hash = await hashPassword(String(b.password), salt);
          await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE nguoi_dung SET password_hash = ?, salt = ? WHERE id = ?")
            .bind(hash, salt, uid)
            .run();
        }
        return json({ ok: true });
      }
      if (userMatch && method === "DELETE") {
        if (!canManageUsers && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "users") && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "staff")) return err("Chi admin/subadmin hoac tai khoan duoc cap module Quan ly kenh ban / Quan ly nguoi dung moi co quyen xoa nguoi dung", 403);
        if (!(hasModule(me, "users") || hasModule(me, "staff"))) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module nay", 403);
        if (Number(userMatch[1]) === me.id) return err("Khong the tu xoa tai khoan dang dang nhap", 422);
        const leadRef = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM nguoi_dung WHERE leader_id = ?").bind(Number(userMatch[1])).first();
        if (leadRef && leadRef.c > 0) return err("Khong the xoa — nguoi dung nay dang la Lanh dao phu trach cua " + leadRef.c + " diem ban. Hay gan lai lanh dao phu trach truoc khi xoa.", 422);
        await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ?").bind(Number(userMatch[1])).run();
        return json({ ok: true });
      }
      // ---- Xoa nhieu nguoi dung cung luc (theo danh sach da danh dau) ----
      if (path === "/api/users/bulk-delete" && method === "POST") {
        if (!canManageUsers && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "users") && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "staff")) return err("Chi admin/subadmin hoac tai khoan duoc cap module Quan ly kenh ban / Quan ly nguoi dung moi co quyen xoa nguoi dung", 403);
        if (!(hasModule(me, "users") || hasModule(me, "staff"))) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module nay", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const ids = [...new Set((Array.isArray(b.ids) ? b.ids : []).map(Number).filter((n) => n > 0))];
        if (!ids.length) return err("Chua chon nguoi dung nao de xoa", 422);
        let deleted = 0;
        const skipped = [];
        for (const id of ids) {
          if (id === me.id) { skipped.push({ id, reason: "Khong the tu xoa tai khoan dang dang nhap" }); continue; }
          const leadRef = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM nguoi_dung WHERE leader_id = ?").bind(id).first();
          if (leadRef && leadRef.c > 0) { skipped.push({ id, reason: "Dang la Lanh dao phu trach cua " + leadRef.c + " diem ban — hay gan lai lanh dao phu trach truoc khi xoa" }); continue; }
          const r = await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ?").bind(id).run();
          if ((r.meta.changes || 0) > 0) deleted++;
          else skipped.push({ id, reason: "Khong tim thay nguoi dung" });
        }
        return json({ ok: true, deleted, skipped });
      }
      // ---- Gan/go quyen module hang loat cho nhan su (muc Quan ly nguoi dung, 15/11) ----
      // Admin chon nhieu user + 1 module, bam Gan hoac Go: 1 lan goi cap nhat het.
      if (path === "/api/users/bulk-modules" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi co quyen gan/go module hang loat", 403);
        if (!hasModule(me, "staff")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module Quan ly nguoi dung", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const module = String(b.module || "").trim();
        if (!MODULE_KEYS.includes(module)) return err("Module khong hop le", 422);
        const kind = b.kind === "pos" ? "pos" : b.kind === "staff" ? "staff" : "";
        const action = b.action === "remove" ? "remove" : "assign";
        const ids = [...new Set((Array.isArray(b.ids) ? b.ids : []).map(Number).filter((n) => n > 0))];
        if (!ids.length) return err("Chua chon nguoi dung nao", 422);
        let updated = 0, unchanged = 0;
        const skipped = [];
        for (const id of ids) {
          const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, role, modules FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ?").bind(id).first();
          if (!row) { skipped.push({ id, reason: "Khong tim thay nguoi dung" }); continue; }
          if (kind === "staff" && row.role === "user") { skipped.push({ id, reason: "Tai khoan diem ban duoc quan ly trong module Quan ly kenh ban" }); continue; }
          if (kind === "pos" && row.role !== "user") { skipped.push({ id, reason: "Chi ap dung cho tai khoan diem ban" }); continue; }
          const mods = parseModules(row.modules);
          if (mods === null) {
            // Tai khoan chua thiet lap rieng: mac dinh dung du quyen theo vai tro.
            if (action === "assign") { unchanged++; continue; } // da co quyen roi
            // Go khoi tai khoan day du quyen: cu the hoa thanh toan bo module tru key nay.
            const next = MODULE_KEYS.filter((k) => k !== module);
            await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE nguoi_dung SET modules = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(JSON.stringify(next), id).run();
            updated++;
            continue;
          }
          const has = mods.includes(module);
          if (action === "assign" && has) { unchanged++; continue; }
          if (action === "remove" && !has) { unchanged++; continue; }
          const next = action === "assign" ? [...mods, module] : mods.filter((k) => k !== module);
          await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE nguoi_dung SET modules = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(JSON.stringify(next), id).run();
          updated++;
        }
        return json({ ok: true, updated, unchanged, skipped });
      }
      // ---- Reset mat khau nhan su ve mac dinh Vnpt@2026 (module Quan ly nguoi dung) ----
      const resetMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/users\/(\d+)\/reset-password$/);
      if (resetMatch && method === "POST") {
        if (!canManageUsers) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi co quyen reset mat khau", 403);
        if (!hasModule(me, "staff")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module Quan ly nguoi dung", 403);
        const rid = Number(resetMatch[1]);
        if (rid === me.id) return err("Khong the reset mat khau chinh minh tai day — doi mat khau trong module Cai dat", 422);
        const target = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, role FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ?").bind(rid).first();
        if (!target) return err("Khong tim thay nguoi dung", 404);
        if (target.role === "user") return err("Tai khoan diem ban duoc quan ly trong module Quan ly kenh ban", 422);
        const salt = randomSalt();
        const hash = await hashPassword("Vnpt@2026", salt);
        await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE nguoi_dung SET password_hash = ?, salt = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(hash, salt, rid).run();
        return json({ ok: true });
      }

      // ---- Quan ly loai du lieu dau vao ----
      if (path === "/api/datatypes" && method === "GET") {
        const types = await getDataTypes(env);
        return json({ ok: true, types });
      }
      if (path === "/api/datatypes" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi co quyen tao loai du lieu", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const code = String(b.code || "").trim().toUpperCase();
        const name = toText(b.name);
        const fmt = b.fmt === "MMYY" ? "MMYY" : "MMYYYY";
        const columns = Array.isArray(b.columns) ? b.columns : [];
        if (!/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{1,11}$/.test(code)) return err("Ma loai du lieu khong hop le (2-12 ky tu, bat dau bang chu cai, chi gom chu/so/_)", 422);
        if (!name) return err("Thieu ten loai du lieu", 422);
        if (!columns.length) return err("Loai du lieu phai co it nhat 1 cot", 422);
        const keys = new Set();
        for (const c of columns) {
          if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(String(c.key || ""))) return err("Khoa cot khong hop le: " + (c.key || ""), 422);
          if (keys.has(c.key)) return err("Trung khoa cot: " + c.key, 422);
          keys.add(c.key);
        }
        // money_col / time_col KHONG bat buoc nua (loai du lieu ban hang co the
        // de "Khong dung") — cac loai thieu 2 cot nay tu dong khong lot vao
        // Dashboard/Bao cao chi tra hoa hong (aggregateSummary da loc san).
        for (const rc of ["eload_col"]) {
          if (!b[rc] || !keys.has(b[rc])) return err("Chua chon cot vai tro bat buoc: " + rc, 422);
        }
        const salesKind = ["doanh_thu", "san_luong"].includes(b.sales_kind) ? b.sales_kind : "";
        for (const rc of ["money_col", "time_col", "time_fallback_col", "vi_col", "sub_col", "pkg_col", "trans_col", "status_col", "reason_col", "key_col", "kenh_col", "sales_unit_col", "sales_eload_col", "sales_kenh_col", "sales_price_col", "sales_qty_col", "sales_result_col", "sales_subtype_col"]) {
          if (b[rc] && !keys.has(b[rc])) return err("Cot vai tro khong nam trong danh sach cot: " + rc, 422);
        }
        const exists = (await getDataTypes(env)).some((t) => t.code === code || t.prefix === code);
        if (exists) return err("Ma loai du lieu da ton tai: " + code, 422);
        const cleanCols = columns.map((c) => ({
          key: String(c.key), label: toText(c.label) || String(c.key),
          type: ["money", "number", "date"].includes(c.type) ? c.type : "text",
        }));
        const r = await env.DB.prepare(
          "INSERT INTO loai_du_lieu (code, name, prefix, fmt, columns_json, money_col, time_col, time_fallback_col, eload_col, vi_col, sub_col, pkg_col, trans_col, status_col, reason_col, key_col, kenh_col, sales_unit_col, sales_eload_col, sales_kenh_col, sales_price_col, sales_qty_col, sales_result_col, sales_subtype_col, sales_kind, builtin) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)"
        )
          .bind(code, name, code, fmt, JSON.stringify(cleanCols),
            b.money_col, b.time_col, b.time_fallback_col || "", b.eload_col, b.vi_col || "",
            b.sub_col || "", b.pkg_col || "", b.trans_col || "", b.status_col || "", b.reason_col || "", b.key_col || "", b.kenh_col || "",
            b.sales_unit_col || "", b.sales_eload_col || "", b.sales_kenh_col || "", b.sales_price_col || "", b.sales_qty_col || "",
            b.sales_result_col || "", b.sales_subtype_col || "", salesKind)
          .run();
        return json({ ok: true, id: r.meta.last_row_id, code });
      }
      // Sua loai du lieu TU TAO (admin): doi duoc ten, danh sach cot va cac
      // cot vai tro. Khong doi duoc ma (dinh danh), dinh dang ten bang
      // (fmt) va khong sua duoc loai mac dinh — doi fmt sau khi da co bang
      // thang se lam mo coi cac bang cu.
      const dtPutMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/datatypes\/(\d+)$/);
      if (dtPutMatch && method === "PUT") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi co quyen sua loai du lieu", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM loai_du_lieu WHERE id = ?").bind(Number(dtPutMatch[1])).first();
        if (!row) return err("Loai du lieu khong ton tai", 404);
        if (row.builtin) return err("Khong sua duoc loai du lieu mac dinh", 403);
        const name = toText(b.name);
        const columns = Array.isArray(b.columns) ? b.columns : [];
        if (!name) return err("Thieu ten loai du lieu", 422);
        if (!columns.length) return err("Loai du lieu phai co it nhat 1 cot", 422);
        const keys = new Set();
        for (const c of columns) {
          if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(String(c.key || ""))) return err("Khoa cot khong hop le: " + (c.key || ""), 422);
          if (keys.has(c.key)) return err("Trung khoa cot: " + c.key, 422);
          keys.add(c.key);
        }
        for (const rc of ["eload_col"]) {
          if (!b[rc] || !keys.has(b[rc])) return err("Chua chon cot vai tro bat buoc: " + rc, 422);
        }
        const salesKind = ["doanh_thu", "san_luong"].includes(b.sales_kind) ? b.sales_kind : "";
        for (const rc of ["money_col", "time_col", "time_fallback_col", "vi_col", "sub_col", "pkg_col", "trans_col", "status_col", "reason_col", "key_col", "kenh_col", "sales_unit_col", "sales_eload_col", "sales_kenh_col", "sales_price_col", "sales_qty_col", "sales_result_col", "sales_subtype_col"]) {
          if (b[rc] && !keys.has(b[rc])) return err("Cot vai tro khong nam trong danh sach cot: " + rc, 422);
        }
        const cleanCols = columns.map((c) => ({
          key: String(c.key), label: toText(c.label) || String(c.key),
          type: ["money", "number", "date"].includes(c.type) ? c.type : "text",
        }));
        await env.DB.prepare(
          "UPDATE loai_du_lieu SET name = ?, columns_json = ?, money_col = ?, time_col = ?, time_fallback_col = ?, eload_col = ?, vi_col = ?, sub_col = ?, pkg_col = ?, trans_col = ?, status_col = ?, reason_col = ?, key_col = ?, kenh_col = ?, sales_unit_col = ?, sales_eload_col = ?, sales_kenh_col = ?, sales_price_col = ?, sales_qty_col = ?, sales_result_col = ?, sales_subtype_col = ?, sales_kind = ? WHERE id = ?"
        )
          .bind(name, JSON.stringify(cleanCols),
            b.money_col, b.time_col, b.time_fallback_col || "", b.eload_col, b.vi_col || "",
            b.sub_col || "", b.pkg_col || "", b.trans_col || "", b.status_col || "", b.reason_col || "", b.key_col || "", b.kenh_col || "",
            b.sales_unit_col || "", b.sales_eload_col || "", b.sales_kenh_col || "", b.sales_price_col || "", b.sales_qty_col || "",
            b.sales_result_col || "", b.sales_subtype_col || "", salesKind, row.id)
          .run();
        return json({ ok: true, code: row.code });
      }
      const dtMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/datatypes\/(\d+)$/);
      if (dtMatch && method === "DELETE") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi co quyen xoa loai du lieu", 403);
        const t = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM loai_du_lieu WHERE id = ?").bind(Number(dtMatch[1])).first();
        if (!t) return err("Khong tim thay loai du lieu", 404);
        if (t.builtin) return err("Khong the xoa loai du lieu mac dinh cua he thong", 422);
        await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM loai_du_lieu WHERE id = ?").bind(Number(dtMatch[1])).run();
        return json({ ok: true });
      }

      // ---- Import du lieu dau vao tu Excel ----
      if (path === "/api/import/tables" && method === "GET") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "import")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module Quan ly du lieu dau vao", 403);
        const types = await getDataTypes(env);
        const out = [];
        for (const t of types) {
          const tables = await listMonthlyTables(env, t.prefix);
          for (const name of tables) {
            const c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM "' + name + '"').first();
            out.push({ table: name, code: t.code, rows: c ? c.n : 0, locked: await isTableLocked(env, name), last_import_at: await lastImportAt(env, name) });
          }
        }
        // Bang danh sach kenh ban / CBCNV theo thang
        for (const [pfx, code] of [[KENH_BAN_PREFIX, "KENH_BAN"], [CBCNV_PREFIX, "CBCNV"]]) {
          const tables = await listMonthlyTables(env, pfx);
          for (const name of tables) {
            const c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM "' + name + '"').first();
            out.push({ table: name, code, rows: c ? c.n : 0, locked: await isTableLocked(env, name), last_import_at: await lastImportAt(env, name) });
          }
        }
        return json({ ok: true, tables: out });
      }
      // ---- Xoa bang du lieu da import ----
      const tblMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/import\/tables\/([A-Za-z0-9_]+)$/);
      if (tblMatch && method === "DELETE") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "import")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module Quan ly du lieu dau vao", 403);
        if (!isAdmin && (isSubAdmin || !me.can_import)) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen xoa bang du lieu (SubAdmin khong duoc phep xoa bang)", 403);
        const name = tblMatch[1];
        const types = await getDataTypes(env);
        const okName = types.some((t) => new RegExp("^" + t.prefix + "_\\d{4,6}$").test(name)) ||
          /^(kenh_ban|cbcnv)_\d{6}$/.test(name);
        if (!okName) return err("Ten bang khong hop le", 422);
        await env.DB.prepare('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "' + name + '"').run();
        return json({ ok: true, table: name });
      }
      // ---- Trang thai khoa thay the cua 1 bang thang ----
      if (path === "/api/import/table-lock" && method === "GET") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "import")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module Quan ly du lieu dau vao", 403);
        const name = url.searchParams.get("table") || "";
        if (!name) return err("Thieu ten bang", 422);
        const locked = await isTableLocked(env, name);
        return json({ ok: true, table: name, locked, can_replace: isAdmin || !locked });
      }
      // ---- Admin khoa / mo khoa thay the cho 1 bang thang ----
      if (path === "/api/table-locks" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdmin) return err("Chi admin moi co quyen khoa/mo khoa bang du lieu", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const name = toText(b.table);
        if (!name) return err("Thieu ten bang", 422);
        await ensureTableLocks(env);
        await env.DB.prepare(
          "INSERT INTO khoa_bang (ten_bang, khoa, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(ten_bang) DO UPDATE SET khoa = excluded.khoa, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at"
        )
          .bind(name, b.locked ? 1 : 0, me.username, saigonNow())
          .run();
        return json({ ok: true, table: name, locked: !!b.locked });
      }
      if (path === "/api/import" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike && !me.can_import && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "import")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen import du lieu", 403);
        if (!hasModule(me, "import")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module Quan ly du lieu dau vao", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const types = await getDataTypes(env);
        const dtype = types.find((t) => t.code === String(b.loai || "").toUpperCase());
        if (!dtype) return err("Loai du lieu khong ton tai: " + (b.loai || ""), 422);
        const mode = b.mode === "replace" ? "replace" : b.mode === "create" ? "create" : "append"; // append = Cap nhat, replace = Thay the, create = Tao moi
        const thang = Number(b.thang);
        const nam = Number(b.nam);
        const columns = typeColumnsSQL(dtype);
        const colNames = columns.map(([n]) => n);
        // Chi giu cac truong khop voi bang map du lieu; dong rong bi loai bo.
        const rawRows = Array.isArray(b.rows) ? b.rows : [];
        const rows = rawRows.filter((row) => {
          if (!row || typeof row !== "object") return false;
          return colNames.some((n) => {
            const v = row[n];
            return v !== null && v !== undefined && String(v).trim() !== "";
          });
        });
        if (!thang || thang < 1 || thang > 12 || !nam) return err("Thang/nam import khong hop le", 422);
        if (!rows.length) return err("Khong co dong du lieu nao de import", 422);
        const table = tableNameForType(dtype, thang, nam);
        // Phan quyen thay the theo tung bang: admin luon duoc phep; user co quyen
        // import chi duoc thay the khi bang KHONG bi khoa (mac dinh: thang hien
        // tai mo, cac thang truoc do khoa tru khi admin mo khoa).
        if (mode === "replace" && !isAdmin) {
          const locked = await isTableLocked(env, table);
          if (locked) return err("Bang du lieu " + table + " dang bi khoa thay the. Chi admin moi duoc thay the du lieu cua bang nay. Lien he admin de mo khoa neu can.", 403);
        }
        if (mode === "create") {
          // Che do Tao moi: chi import khi bang thang nay chua ton tai,
          // tranh import trung lam nhan doi so lieu.
          const ex = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name = ?").bind(table).first();
          if (ex) return err("Bang du lieu " + table + " da ton tai. Hay dung che do 'Cap nhat' de nap them du lieu, hoac 'Thay the' de xoa du lieu cu va nap lai tu dau.", 409);
        }
        if (mode === "replace") {
          await env.DB.prepare('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "' + table + '"').run();
        }
        await env.DB.prepare(createTableSQL(table, columns)).run();
        await ensureListColumns(env, table, columns);
        const numericCols = new Set(columns.filter(([, t]) => t === "REAL").map(([n]) => n));
        const sql =
          'INSERT INTO "' + table + '" (' + colNames.map((n) => '"' + n + '"').join(", ") +
          ", imported_by) VALUES (" + colNames.map(() => "?").join(", ") + ", ?)";
        let inserted = 0;
        const CHUNK = 100;
        for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += CHUNK) {
          const batch = rows.slice(i, i + CHUNK).map((row) => {
            const vals = colNames.map((n) => {
              const v = row[n];
              return numericCols.has(n) ? toNumber(v) : toText(v);
            });
            vals.push(me.username);
            return env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...vals);
          });
          await env.DB.batch(batch);
          inserted += batch.length;
        }
        return json({ ok: true, table, inserted, mode });
      }

            // ---- Import du lieu TTCSKH (module 5.9 "Import du lieu TTCSKH") ----
      // 9 loai du lieu dau vao cho Dashboard TTCSKH, lay truong dung theo file
      // Excel anh gui: 5 loai KQTH OB (13/11, file chi co hang tieu de) + 4 loai
      // Tong hop Autocall (14/11, file co 1 dong du lieu mau QNH). IPCC sua lai
      // theo file moi 14/11: chi con 20 cot (bo 8 cot "So TB duoc goi N lan" bi
      // trung ten). Moi thang 1 bang PREFIX_MMYYYY; 3 che do nhu import chung:
      // Tao moi (409 neu bang da co), Cap nhat (nap them dong vao bang da co),
      // Thay the (xoa bang roi nap lai). Map cot THEO VI TRI (khong theo ten)
      // vi file CKN co 2 cot "Chu ky goi" (cot 10: truoc OB; cot 20: goi moi).
      // NGOAI LE: IPCC map THEO TEN (14/11, theo yeu cau anh) vi file du lieu
      // IPCC da bi xoa bot cot — trung ten thi map, khong trung thi bo qua.
      // So dung toNumberTtcskh (khong dung toNumber chung) vi file TH co o text
      // dang "62.35%" — dau . la thap phan, khong phai hang nghin.
      // Parse so cho import TTCSKH: o so Excel truyen thang; o text co the la
      // "62.35%" (dau . la thap phan, kieu file TH Autocall) hoac "150.000"
      // (dau . la hang nghin, dinh dang VN). toNumber() chung luon hieu dau .
      // la hang nghin nen se bien "62.35%" thanh 6235 — sai 100 lan.
      const toNumberTtcskh = (v) => {
        if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return 0;
        if (typeof v === "number") return Number.isNaN(v) ? 0 : v;
        let str = String(v).trim().replace(/%/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
        if (!str) return 0;
        if (str.includes(",")) return toNumber(str); // "1.234,56" / "62,35": VN
        if (/^-?\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})+$/.test(str)) return toNumber(str); // "150.000"
        const n = parseFloat(str.replace(/[^0-9.\-]/g, ""));
        return Number.isNaN(n) ? 0 : n;
      };
      const TTCSKH_TYPES = [
        { code: "OBCKN", name: "KQTH OB gia hạn CKN", prefix: "OBCKN", columns: [
          ["so_thue_bao", "TEXT", "Số thuê bao"], ["ngay_kich_hoat", "TEXT", "Ngày kích hoạt"], ["ten_chuong_trinh", "TEXT", "Tên chương trình"], ["tinh", "TEXT", "Tỉnh"], ["ma_tinh_cu", "TEXT", "Mã tỉnh cũ"], ["user_dtv", "TEXT", "User ĐTV"], ["ma_hrm", "TEXT", "Mã HRM"], ["phong_ban", "TEXT", "Phòng ban"], ["ma_goi_truoc_ob", "TEXT", "Mã gói trước khi OB"], ["chu_ky_goi", "TEXT", "Chu kỳ gói (trước OB)"], ["doanh_thu_goi_truoc_gh", "REAL", "Doanh thu gói trước khi gia hạn"], ["trang_thai_ob", "TEXT", "Trạng thái OB"], ["ll_duoc_dong_y_dk", "TEXT", "Liên lạc được-KH đồng ý Đk"], ["ll_duoc_chua_co_nhu_cau", "TEXT", "Liên lạc được-KH chưa có nhu cầu sd gói"], ["ll_duoc_goi_cuoc_chua_phu_hop", "TEXT", "Liên lạc được-gói cước chưa phù hợp"], ["ll_duoc_dang_dung_sim_mang_khac", "TEXT", "Liên lạc được-KH đang dùng SIM có gói của mạng khác"], ["ll_duoc_hen_goi_lai", "TEXT", "Liên lạc được-KH hẹn gọi lại"], ["thoi_gian_thuc_hien_ob", "TEXT", "Thời gian thực hiện OB"], ["ma_goi_dv_moi", "TEXT", "Mã gói dịch vụ mới"], ["chu_ky_goi_moi", "TEXT", "Chu kỳ gói (gói mới)"], ["doanh_thu_dk_gh", "REAL", "Doanh thu ĐK/GH"], ["chu_ky_gia_han", "TEXT", "Chu kỳ gia hạn"], ["nghiep_vu_goi", "TEXT", "Nghiệp vụ gói"], ["ngay_mo_dv", "TEXT", "Ngày mở dịch vụ"], ["nguon_dk_gh", "TEXT", "Nguồn ĐK/GH"], ["gh_dk_tc_ccos_ob", "TEXT", "Gia hạn/đăng ký thành công qua CCOS - OB"], ["gia_han_tu_dong", "TEXT", "Gia hạn tự động"], ["tb_chuyen_doi_goi", "TEXT", "TB chuyển đổi gói"], ["tb_chuyen_doi_goi_tc", "TEXT", "TB chuyển đổi gói thành công"], ["loai_thue_bao", "TEXT", "Loại thuê bao"], ["tb_truyen_thong_5g", "TEXT", "TB được truyền thông 5G"],

      const normCareStatus = (v) => String(v == null ? "" : v).trim().toLowerCase().normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").replace(/đ/g, "d").replace(/\s+/g, " ");

      const normCareDate = (v) => {
        if (v == null || v === "") return "";
        if (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v)) {
          const d = new Date(Math.round((v - 25569) * 86400 * 1000));
          return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? "" : d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        }
        const s = String(v).trim();
        let m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
        if (m) return m[1] + "-" + m[2] + "-" + m[3];
        m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/);
        if (m) return m[3] + "-" + m[2].padStart(2, "0") + "-" + m[1].padStart(2, "0");
        const d = new Date(s);
        return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? "" : d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

        const inScope = (duid) => scopeUnitIds === null || (duid != null && scopeUnitIds.includes(duid));
        const normE2 = (s) => String(s == null ? "" : s).trim().toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, "");
        const resolveType = (hang, loai) => {
          const h = normLoaiKenh(hang);
          if (h === "DUQ" || h === "DBL") return h;
          return normLoaiKenh(loai);
        };
        // Vu tru diem ban: bang kenh_ban thang gan nhat trong ky + diem chi co
        // trong file CS (26/11/2026, anh Tuan: tong kenh la 480 dong kenh_ban;
        // tai khoan diem ban KHONG cong vao mau so vi ma kenh tai khoan dang
        // KBH... khac ma kenh_ban QNH... nen cong trung thanh 655 diem).
        const posMap = new Map();
        const eloadToKey = new Map();
        const codeAlias = new Map();
        const kbCodeToKey = new Map(); // ma_kenh trong bang kenh_ban -> key
        const registerKeys = (key, code, el) => {
          if (code) codeAlias.set("mk:" + code, key);
          if (el) eloadToKey.set(el, key);
        };
        const resolveKey = (code, el) => {
          if (code && codeAlias.has("mk:" + code)) return codeAlias.get("mk:" + code);
          if (el && eloadToKey.has(el)) return eloadToKey.get(el);
          if (code && posMap.has("mk:" + code)) return "mk:" + code;
          return null;
        };
        const newPos = (key, p) => {
          const o = { code: "", eload: "", don_vi_id: null, ten_don_vi: "", type: "", mgr: "", mgrTen: "", days: {}, ...p };
          posMap.set(key, o);
          registerKeys(key, o.code, o.eload);
          return o;
        };
        // (1) Bang kenh_ban thang gan nhat trong ky: vu tru mau so + ma NV
        // quan ly + ten NV (ten_nv_bh).
        for (const mk of [...curMonths].reverse()) {
          const t = await monthTableExists(env, KENH_BAN_PREFIX, mk);
          if (!t) continue;
          const kbRows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM "' + t + '"').all()).results || [];
          for (const r of kbRows) {
            const code = normE2(r.ma_kenh);
            const el = normE2(r.so_eload);
            let key = resolveKey(code, el);
            const duid = r.don_vi_id != null && String(r.don_vi_id).trim() !== "" ? Number(r.don_vi_id) : (unitNameToId[normU(r.don_vi)] || null);
            const tp = resolveType(r.hang_kenh, r.loai_kenh);
            if (!key) {
              key = code ? "mk:" + code : (el ? "el:" + el : null);
              if (!key) continue;
              newPos(key, { code, eload: el, don_vi_id: duid, ten_don_vi: duid != null ? (duidToName[duid] || "") : (r.don_vi || ""), type: tp, mgr: normE2(r.ma_nv_quan_ly), mgrTen: r.ten_nv_bh ? String(r.ten_nv_bh).trim() : "" });
              if (code) kbCodeToKey.set(code, key);
            } else {
              const p = posMap.get(key);
              registerKeys(key, code, el);
              if (code) kbCodeToKey.set(code, key);
              if (!p.type && tp) p.type = tp;
              if (p.don_vi_id == null && duid != null) { p.don_vi_id = duid; p.ten_don_vi = duidToName[duid] || r.don_vi || p.ten_don_vi; }
              if (!p.mgr && normE2(r.ma_nv_quan_ly)) p.mgr = normE2(r.ma_nv_quan_ly);
              if (!p.mgrTen && r.ten_nv_bh) p.mgrTen = String(r.ten_nv_bh).trim();
            }
          }
          break;
        }
        // (3) Ten NV quan ly tu bang cbcnv thang gan nhat trong ky
        const mgrName = new Map();
        for (const mk of [...curMonths].reverse()) {
          const t = await monthTableExists(env, CBCNV_PREFIX, mk);
          if (!t) continue;
          const mrs = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM "' + t + '"').all()).results || [];
          for (const m of mrs) {
            const nm = String(m.ho_ten || "").trim();
            if (!nm) continue;
            for (const col of ["ma_smcs", "ma_hrm", "user_ccbs"]) {
              const k = normE2(m[col]);
              if (k && !mgrName.has(k)) mgrName.set(k, nm);
            }
          }
          break;
        }
        // (4) Dong cham soc: MOI dong deu tinh (26/11/2026, anh Tuan: du lieu
        // import 100% hop le), dem theo ngay khac nhau; chi bo dong khong doc
        // duoc ngay (khong xep vao thang nao duoc).
        let tables = [];
        let careRows = 0, validRows = 0;
        const diag = { matched_kb: 0, matched_phone: 0, matched_code: 0, matched_new: 0, rows_no_key: 0, date_bad: 0, status_top: {} };
        for (const mk of curMonths) {
          const t = await monthTableExists(env, CSKBH_PREFIX, mk);
          if (!t) continue;
          tables.push(t);
          const rows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM "' + t + '"').all()).results || [];
          for (const r of rows) {
            careRows++;
            const code = normE2(r.ma_diem_ban);
            const el = normE2(r.sdt_diem_ban);
            const nvCode = normE2(r.ma_hrm_nv);
            if (!code && !el) { diag.rows_no_key++; continue; }
            // Join chinh: ma_diem_ban (CSKBH) <-> ma_kenh (bang kenh_ban),
            // roi ma tai khoan, cuoi cung SDT (26/11/2026, anh Tuan).
            let key = null, matchHow = "";
            if (code && kbCodeToKey.has(code)) { key = kbCodeToKey.get(code); matchHow = "kb"; }
            else if (el && eloadToKey.has(el)) { key = eloadToKey.get(el); matchHow = "phone"; }
            else if (code && posMap.has("mk:" + code)) { key = "mk:" + code; matchHow = "code"; }
            let p = key ? posMap.get(key) : null;
            if (p) { if (matchHow === "kb") diag.matched_kb++; else if (matchHow === "phone") diag.matched_phone++; else diag.matched_code++; }
            else diag.matched_new++;
            if (!p) {
              key = code ? "mk:" + code : "el:" + el;
              const duid = unitNameToId[normU(r.phong_bhkv)] || null;
              p = newPos(key, { code, eload: el, don_vi_id: duid, ten_don_vi: duid != null ? (duidToName[duid] || "") : (r.phong_bhkv || ""), type: normLoaiKenh(r.loai_diem_ban), mgr: nvCode, mgrTen: r.nhan_vien_quan_ly ? String(r.nhan_vien_quan_ly).trim() : "" });
            } else {
              registerKeys(key, code, el);
            }
            if (!p.mgr && nvCode) { p.mgr = nvCode; p.mgrTen = r.nhan_vien_quan_ly ? String(r.nhan_vien_quan_ly).trim() : p.mgrTen; }
            if (!p.type) p.type = normLoaiKenh(r.loai_diem_ban);
            if (p.don_vi_id == null) {
              const duid = unitNameToId[normU(r.phong_bhkv)] || null;
              if (duid != null) { p.don_vi_id = duid; p.ten_don_vi = duidToName[duid] || p.ten_don_vi; }
            }
            const stN = normCareStatus(r.trang_thai_cham_soc);
            const stK = stN || "(trong)";
            diag.status_top[stK] = (diag.status_top[stK] || 0) + 1;
            const day = normCareDate(r.ngay_cham_soc);
            if (!day) { diag.date_bad++; continue; }
            validRows++;
            const mm = day.slice(0, 7);
            if (!p.days[mm]) p.days[mm] = new Set();
            p.days[mm].add(day);
          }
        }
        // Dat chuan trong ky: DUQ >= 2 ngay khac nhau o it nhat 1 thang; DBL >= 1 ngay
        const isCared = (p) => {
          if (p.type === "DUQ") return curMonths.some((mk) => (p.days[mk] ? p.days[mk].size : 0) >= 2);
          if (p.type === "DBL") return curMonths.some((mk) => (p.days[mk] ? p.days[mk].size : 0) >= 1);
          return false;
        };
        const rate1 = (a, b) => b > 0 ? Math.round(a / b * 1000) / 10 : null;
        const byUnitMap = new Map();
        const byNvMap = new Map();
        let posNoMgr = 0, posUnclassified = 0;
        for (const p of posMap.values()) {
          if (!inScope(p.don_vi_id)) continue;
          if (p.type !== "DUQ" && p.type !== "DBL") { posUnclassified++; continue; }
          const cared = isCared(p);
          const ukey = p.don_vi_id != null ? "id:" + p.don_vi_id : "ten:" + normU(p.ten_don_vi || "Chưa xác định");
          const u = byUnitMap.get(ukey) || { don_vi_id: p.don_vi_id, ten: p.don_vi_id != null ? (duidToName[p.don_vi_id] || p.ten_don_vi || "Đơn vị " + p.don_vi_id) : (p.ten_don_vi || "Chưa xác định"), sl_duq: 0, sl_duq_cs: 0, sl_dbl: 0, sl_dbl_cs: 0 };
          if (p.type === "DUQ") { u.sl_duq++; if (cared) u.sl_duq_cs++; } else { u.sl_dbl++; if (cared) u.sl_dbl_cs++; }
          byUnitMap.set(ukey, u);
          if (!p.mgr) { posNoMgr++; continue; }
          const nv = byNvMap.get(p.mgr) || { ma_nv: p.mgr, ten_nv: mgrName.get(p.mgr) || p.mgrTen || p.mgr, don_vi_id: p.don_vi_id, ten_don_vi: u.ten, sl_duq: 0, sl_duq_cs: 0, sl_dbl: 0, sl_dbl_cs: 0, _units: {} };
          if (p.type === "DUQ") { nv.sl_duq++; if (cared) nv.sl_duq_cs++; } else { nv.sl_dbl++; if (cared) nv.sl_dbl_cs++; }
          const uk2 = p.don_vi_id != null ? p.don_vi_id : "x";
          nv._units[uk2] = (nv._units[uk2] || 0) + 1;
          byNvMap.set(p.mgr, nv);
        }
        // Don vi cua NV = don vi co nhieu diem quan ly nhat (trong pham vi)
        for (const nv of byNvMap.values()) {
          let best = null, bestN = -1;
          for (const k in nv._units) { if (nv._units[k] > bestN) { bestN = nv._units[k]; best = k; } }
          if (best !== null && best !== "x") { nv.don_vi_id = Number(best); nv.ten_don_vi = duidToName[nv.don_vi_id] || nv.ten_don_vi; }
          delete nv._units;
        }
        const by_unit = [...byUnitMap.values()].map((u) => ({ ...u, ty_le_duq: rate1(u.sl_duq_cs, u.sl_duq), ty_le_dbl: rate1(u.sl_dbl_cs, u.sl_dbl) }));
        // Chi liet ke NV duoc giao quan ly it nhat 1 diem DUQ/DBL (26/11/2026,
        // anh Tuan: NV khong co diem ban thi an di). NV chi co diem DDV hoac
        // chua phan loai da bi loai o vong lap tren, day la lop chan them.
        const by_nv = [...byNvMap.values()].filter((nv) => (nv.sl_duq || 0) + (nv.sl_dbl || 0) > 0).map((nv) => ({ ...nv, ty_le_duq: rate1(nv.sl_duq_cs, nv.sl_duq), ty_le_dbl: rate1(nv.sl_dbl_cs, nv.sl_dbl) })).sort((a, b) => String(a.ten_don_vi).localeCompare(String(b.ten_don_vi), "vi") || String(a.ten_nv).localeCompare(String(b.ten_nv), "vi"));
        const tong = by_unit.reduce((t, u) => ({ sl_duq: t.sl_duq + u.sl_duq, sl_duq_cs: t.sl_duq_cs + u.sl_duq_cs, sl_dbl: t.sl_dbl + u.sl_dbl, sl_dbl_cs: t.sl_dbl_cs + u.sl_dbl_cs }), { sl_duq: 0, sl_duq_cs: 0, sl_dbl: 0, sl_dbl_cs: 0 });
        tong.ty_le_duq = rate1(tong.sl_duq_cs, tong.sl_duq);
        tong.ty_le_dbl = rate1(tong.sl_dbl_cs, tong.sl_dbl);
        const statusTop = Object.entries(diag.status_top).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 6);
        return json({ ok: true, ky, thang: ky === "thang" ? curMonths[0] : null, nam: ky === "nam" ? nam : null, period_label: periodLabel, has_data: tables.length > 0, tables, care_rows: careRows, valid_rows: validRows, by_unit, by_nv, tong, meta: { pos_no_mgr: posNoMgr, pos_unclassified: posUnclassified }, diag: { tables, care_rows: careRows, valid_rows: validRows, matched_kb_code: diag.matched_kb, matched_phone: diag.matched_phone, matched_code_other: diag.matched_code, matched_new: diag.matched_new, rows_no_key: diag.rows_no_key, date_bad: diag.date_bad, status_top: statusTop } });
      }

      // ---- Dong bo du lieu tu Google Sheet (module 5.10) ----
      // 11 mon: SMCS + TNTTTB (cot lay tu loai_du_lieu, muc 5.6) + 9 loai
      // TTCSKH (cot co dinh nhu module 5.9). Chi dong bo vao bang cua THANG
      // HIEN TAI, che do thay the toan bo; bang chua co thi tu tao moi.
      const GSHEET_ITEMS = [
        { key: "SMCS", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng SMCS", kind: "datatype" },
        { key: "TNTTTB", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng TNTTTB", kind: "datatype" },
        { key: "OBCKN", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng chi tiết OBCKN", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "OBCKD", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng chi tiết OBCKD", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "OBBG", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng chi tiết OBBG", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "OBHVC", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng chi tiết OBHVC", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "THCKN", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng THCKN", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "THCKD", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng THCKD", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "THBG", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng THBG", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "THHVC", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng THHVC", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "IPCC", name: "Dữ liệu đầu vào bảng IPCC", kind: "ttcskh" },
        { key: "CSKBH", name: "Kết quả chăm sóc kênh bán hàng", kind: "cskbh" },
      ];
      // Cot dich + ten bang thang hien tai cua 1 mon. Tra {error} neu loai
      // SMCS/TNTTTB chua duoc cau hinh o muc 5.6.
      const gsheetTarget = async (env, key) => {
        const item = GSHEET_ITEMS.find((x) => x.key === key);
        if (!item) return { error: "Mon dong bo khong ton tai: " + key };
        const month = saigonNow().slice(0, 7);
        if (item.kind === "ttcskh") {
          const t = TTCSKH_TYPES.find((x) => x.code === key);
          return { item, month, prefix: t.prefix, name: t.name, table: monthTableName(t.prefix, month), columns: t.columns.map(([k, ty, lb]) => ({ key: k, type: ty, label: lb })) };
        }
        if (item.kind === "cskbh") {
          return { item, month, prefix: CSKBH_PREFIX, name: "Kết quả chăm sóc kênh bán hàng", table: monthTableName(CSKBH_PREFIX, month), columns: CSKBH_COLUMNS.map(([k, ty, lb]) => ({ key: k, type: ty, label: lb })) };
        }
        const types = await getDataTypes(env);
        const dt = types.find((t) => t.code === key);
        if (!dt) return { item, month, error: "Chua cau hinh loai du lieu " + key + " o muc 5.6 (Quan ly loai du lieu). Hay tao loai du lieu nay truoc roi quay lai day." };
        return { item, month, prefix: dt.prefix, name: dt.name, table: monthTableName(dt.prefix, month), columns: (dt.columns || []).map((c) => ({ key: c.key, type: c.type === "money" || c.type === "number" ? "REAL" : "TEXT", label: c.label })) };
      };
      // Doc cau hinh da luu cua 1 mon (null neu chua cau hinh).
      const gsheetReadConfig = async (env, key) => {
        await ensureGsheetConfig(env);
        const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM gsheet_sync_config WHERE data_key = ?").bind(key).first();
        if (!r) return null;
        let colMap = null;
        try { colMap = r.col_map ? JSON.parse(r.col_map) : null; } catch (e) { colMap = null; }
        return { key: r.data_key, sheet_url: r.sheet_url || "", gid: r.gid || "0", start_col: r.start_col || "", start_row: r.start_row, end_col: r.end_col || "", end_row: r.end_row, col_map: colMap, updated_at: r.updated_at, last_sync_at: r.last_sync_at, last_sync_rows: r.last_sync_rows, last_sync_status: r.last_sync_status, last_sync_msg: r.last_sync_msg };
      };
      const gsheetWriteSyncStatus = async (env, key, status, rows, msg) => {
        await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE gsheet_sync_config SET last_sync_at = ?, last_sync_rows = ?, last_sync_status = ?, last_sync_msg = ? WHERE data_key = ?").bind(saigonNow(), rows, status, msg || "", key).run();
      };
      const gsheetErr = (message, status) => Object.assign(new Error(message), { status });
      // Dong bo 1 mon: tai sheet -> ap vung/map -> thay the bang thang hien tai.
      const gsheetSyncOne = async (env, meUser, key) => {
