        const target = await gsheetTarget(env, key);
        if (target.error) throw gsheetErr(target.error, 422);
        const cfg = await gsheetReadConfig(env, key);
        if (!cfg || !cfg.sheet_url) throw gsheetErr("Mon " + key + " chua cau hinh link Google Sheet. Hay nhap link, xem truoc va luu cau hinh truoc.", 422);
        let grid;
        try { grid = await fetchSheetGrid(cfg.sheet_url, cfg.gid); }
        catch (e) { throw gsheetErr(e.message, 422); }
        let ranged;
        try { ranged = applyGsheetRange(grid, cfg); }
        catch (e) { throw gsheetErr(e.message, 422); }
        const columns = target.columns.map((c) => [c.key, c.type]);
        const colNames = columns.map(([n]) => n);
        const map = (cfg.col_map && typeof cfg.col_map === "object" && Object.keys(cfg.col_map).length)
          ? cfg.col_map
          : suggestGsheetMap(ranged.headers, target.columns.map((c) => [c.key, c.type, c.label]));
        // Chuyen dong sheet -> dong DB theo map (cot khong map thi bo qua,
        // cot DB khong map thi de trong); dong rong hoan toan thi bo.
        const dbRows = [];
        for (const r of ranged.rows) {
          const obj = {};
          let any = false;
          for (const idx in map) {
            const dbCol = map[idx];
            if (!colNames.includes(dbCol)) continue;
            const v = r[Number(idx)];
            if (v !== undefined && v !== null && String(v).trim() !== "") { obj[dbCol] = v; any = true; }
          }
          if (any) dbRows.push(obj);
        }
        if (!dbRows.length) throw gsheetErr("Sheet khong co dong du lieu nao sau khi ap vung va map cot (tieu de: " + ranged.headers.filter((h) => String(h).trim() !== "").join(", ") + ").", 422);
        if (!isAdmin) {
          const locked = await isTableLocked(env, target.table);
          if (locked) throw gsheetErr("Bang " + target.table + " dang bi khoa thay the. Chi admin moi duoc dong bo thay the bang nay. Lien he admin de mo khoa neu can.", 403);
        }
        await env.DB.prepare('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "' + target.table + '"').run();
        await env.DB.prepare(createTableSQL(target.table, columns)).run();
        await ensureListColumns(env, target.table, columns);
        const numericCols = new Set(columns.filter(([, t]) => t === "REAL").map(([n]) => n));
        const toNum = target.item.kind === "ttcskh" ? toNumberTtcskh : toNumber;
        const sql =
          'INSERT INTO "' + target.table + '" (' + colNames.map((n) => '"' + n + '"').join(", ") +
          ", imported_by) VALUES (" + colNames.map(() => "?").join(", ") + ", ?)";
        let inserted = 0;
        const CHUNK = 100;
        for (let i = 0; i < dbRows.length; i += CHUNK) {
          const batch = dbRows.slice(i, i + CHUNK).map((row) => {
            const vals = colNames.map((n) => {
              const v = row[n];
              return numericCols.has(n) ? toNum(v) : toText(v);
            });
            vals.push(meUser.username);
            return env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...vals);
          });
          await env.DB.batch(batch);
          inserted += batch.length;
        }
        await gsheetWriteSyncStatus(env, key, "ok", inserted, "Dong bo thanh cong " + inserted + " dong vao " + target.table);
        return { key, table: target.table, inserted };
      };
      if (path === "/api/gsheet/config" && method === "GET") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc su dung module Dong bo Google Sheet", 403);
        await ensureGsheetConfig(env);
        const month = saigonNow().slice(0, 7);
        const items = [];
        for (const it of GSHEET_ITEMS) {
          const target = await gsheetTarget(env, it.key);
          const cfg = await gsheetReadConfig(env, it.key);
          let table = null, exists = false, rows = 0;
          if (!target.error) {
            table = target.table;
            const ex = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name = ?").bind(table).first();
            exists = !!ex;
            if (exists) {
              const c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM "' + table + '"').first();
              rows = c ? c.n : 0;
            }
          }
          items.push({ key: it.key, name: it.name, kind: it.kind, error: target.error || null, columns: target.error ? [] : target.columns, table, exists, rows, config: cfg });
        }
        return json({ ok: true, month, items });
      }
      if (path === "/api/gsheet/config" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc su dung module Dong bo Google Sheet", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const key = String(b.key || "").toUpperCase();
        if (!GSHEET_ITEMS.some((x) => x.key === key)) return err("Mon dong bo khong ton tai: " + (b.key || ""), 422);
        const sheetUrl = toText(b.sheet_url);
        if (!sheetUrl) return err("Chua nhap link Google Sheet", 422);
        let parsed;
        try { parsed = parseSheetLink(sheetUrl); } catch (e) { return err(e.message, 422); }
        try { parseGsheetRange(b); } catch (e) { return err(e.message, 422); }
        const gid = toText(b.gid) || parsed.gid;
        const target = await gsheetTarget(env, key);
        let colMap = null;
        if (b.col_map && typeof b.col_map === "object") {
          const known = target.error ? null : new Set(target.columns.map((c) => c.key));
          colMap = {};
          for (const idx in b.col_map) {
            const v = String(b.col_map[idx] || "");
            if (!/^\d+$/.test(String(idx)) || !v) continue;
            if (known && !known.has(v)) continue;
            colMap[idx] = v;
          }
        }
        await ensureGsheetConfig(env);
        await env.DB.prepare(
          "INSERT INTO gsheet_sync_config (data_key, sheet_url, gid, start_col, start_row, end_col, end_row, col_map, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(data_key) DO UPDATE SET sheet_url = excluded.sheet_url, gid = excluded.gid, start_col = excluded.start_col, start_row = excluded.start_row, end_col = excluded.end_col, end_row = excluded.end_row, col_map = COALESCE(excluded.col_map, gsheet_sync_config.col_map), updated_at = excluded.updated_at"
        )
          .bind(key, sheetUrl, gid, toText(b.start_col) || null, b.start_row === "" || b.start_row == null ? null : Number(b.start_row), toText(b.end_col) || null, b.end_row === "" || b.end_row == null ? null : Number(b.end_row), colMap ? JSON.stringify(colMap) : null, saigonNow())
          .run();
        return json({ ok: true, key });
      }
      if (path === "/api/gsheet/preview" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc su dung module Dong bo Google Sheet", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const key = String(b.key || "").toUpperCase();
        const target = await gsheetTarget(env, key);
        if (target.error) return err(target.error, 422);
        const sheetUrl = toText(b.sheet_url);
        if (!sheetUrl) return err("Chua nhap link Google Sheet", 422);
        try {
          const grid = await fetchSheetGrid(sheetUrl, toText(b.gid));
          const ranged = applyGsheetRange(grid, b);
          return json({ ok: true, key, headers: ranged.headers, sample: ranged.rows.slice(0, 10), total_rows: ranged.totalRows, total_cols: ranged.totalCols, suggested_map: suggestGsheetMap(ranged.headers, target.columns.map((c) => [c.key, c.type, c.label])), columns: target.columns });
        } catch (e) { return err(e.message, 422); }
      }
      if (path === "/api/gsheet/sync" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc su dung module Dong bo Google Sheet", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const key = String(b.key || "").toUpperCase();
        try {
          const r = await gsheetSyncOne(env, me, key);
          return json({ ok: true, ...r });
        } catch (e) {
          try { await gsheetWriteSyncStatus(env, key, "error", null, e.message); } catch (e2) { /* bo qua */ }
          return err(e.message, e.status || 500);
        }
      }
      if (path === "/api/gsheet/sync-all" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc su dung module Dong bo Google Sheet", 403);
        const results = [];
        for (const it of GSHEET_ITEMS) {
          const cfg = await gsheetReadConfig(env, it.key);
          if (!cfg || !cfg.sheet_url) { results.push({ key: it.key, ok: false, skipped: true, error: "Chua cau hinh" }); continue; }
          try {
            const r = await gsheetSyncOne(env, me, it.key);
            results.push({ key: it.key, ok: true, table: r.table, inserted: r.inserted });
          } catch (e) {
            try { await gsheetWriteSyncStatus(env, it.key, "error", null, e.message); } catch (e2) { /* bo qua */ }
            results.push({ key: it.key, ok: false, error: e.message });
          }
        }
        return json({ ok: true, results });
      }

// ---- Dinh nghia loai kenh ban (module 5.7 "Cau hinh loai kenh ban") ----
// Admin dinh nghia: Loai diem ban (nhu trong file danh sach kenh) -> Hang kenh
// (VD: ĐUQ, ĐBL, Chuoi, ĐGD, GARA, ĐDV). He thong tra bang nay khi import
// danh sach kenh ban de xac dinh hang kenh cua tung diem ban.
async function ensureLoaikenh(env) {
  await env.DB.prepare(
    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS loai_kenh_ban (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, loai_diem_ban TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, hang_kenh TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, updated_at TEXT)"
  ).run();
}
// Chuan hoa khoa tra cuu (khong dau, khong phan biet hoa/thuong) — dung chung
// cho bang dinh nghia va gia tri "Loai diem ban" trong file kenh.
function normKbKey(s) {
  return String(s == null ? "" : s).trim().toLowerCase()
    .normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").replace(/đ/g, "d")
    .replace(/[–—−‐‒]/g, "-").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/\s*-\s*/g, "-");
}

// ---- Import danh sach kenh ban / CBCNV theo thang ----
      // Moi thang 1 bang: kenh_ban_MMYYYY / cbcnv_MMYYYY. Client tu map cot
      // Excel ve truong chuan va loai bo cot khong lien quan truoc khi gui;
      // server chi nhan cac truong chuan, tu giai don_vi -> don_vi_id.
      if ((path === "/api/kenh-ban/import" || path === "/api/cbcnv/import") && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike && !me.can_import && !hasModuleExplicit(me, "import")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen import du lieu", 403);
        if (!hasModule(me, "import")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen su dung module Quan ly du lieu dau vao", 403);
        const isKenh = path === "/api/kenh-ban/import";
        const prefix = isKenh ? KENH_BAN_PREFIX : CBCNV_PREFIX;
        const label = isKenh ? "danh sach kenh ban" : "danh sach CBCNV";
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const mode = b.mode === "replace" ? "replace" : b.mode === "create" ? "create" : "append";
        const thang = Number(b.thang);
        const nam = Number(b.nam);
        if (!thang || thang < 1 || thang > 12 || !nam) return err("Thang/nam import khong hop le", 422);
        const table = prefix + "_" + String(thang).padStart(2, "0") + nam;
        const columns = isKenh ? KENH_BAN_COLS : CBCNV_COLS;
        const colNames = columns.map(([n]) => n);
        const rawRows = Array.isArray(b.rows) ? b.rows : [];
        let rows = rawRows.filter((row) => {
          if (!row || typeof row !== "object") return false;
          return colNames.some((n) => {
            const v = row[n];
            return v !== null && v !== undefined && String(v).trim() !== "";
          });
        });
        // Kenh ban: chi import dong co "ID diem ban HRM" (id_hrm) — dong
        // thieu ID nay bi bo qua hoan toan, khong ghi vao CSDL.
        let skippedEmptyId = 0;
        if (isKenh) {
          const kept = [];
          for (const row of rows) {
            const idv = row.id_hrm;
            if (idv === undefined || idv === null || String(idv).trim() === "") { skippedEmptyId++; continue; }
            kept.push(row);
          }
          rows = kept;
        }
        if (!rows.length) return err(skippedEmptyId > 0 ? "Tat ca " + skippedEmptyId + " dong deu thieu ID diem ban HRM nen khong co dong nao de import" : "Khong co dong du lieu nao de import", 422);
        if (mode === "replace" && !isAdmin) {
          const locked = await isTableLocked(env, table);
          if (locked) return err("Bang " + table + " dang bi khoa thay the. Chi admin moi duoc thay the. Lien he admin de mo khoa neu can.", 403);
        }
        if (mode === "create") {
          const ex = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name = ?").bind(table).first();
          if (ex) return err("Bang " + label + " thang " + thang + "/" + nam + " da ton tai. Hay dung che do 'Cap nhat' de nap them, hoac 'Thay the' de nap lai tu dau.", 409);
        }
        if (mode === "replace") {
          await env.DB.prepare('DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "' + table + '"').run();
        }
        await env.DB.prepare(createTableSQL(table, columns)).run();
        await ensureListColumns(env, table, columns);
        // Giai ten don vi -> don_vi_id (khong dau, khong phan biet hoa/thuong).
        // Khop chinh xac truoc; neu khong co thi khop "ten don vi chua cum tu"
        // (VD: "Van Don" -> "VNPT Van Don - Co To", "Dia ban" ghi "Ha Long"
        // -> "VNPT Ha Long") vi file kenh/CBCNV ghi quan/huyen/dia ban.
        const normU = (s) => String(s == null ? "" : s).trim().toLowerCase()
          .normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").replace(/đ/g, "d")
          .replace(/[–—−‐‒]/g, "-").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/\s*-\s*/g, "-");
        const units = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, ten_don_vi FROM don_vi").all()).results || [];
        const unitMap = {};
        for (const u of units) unitMap[normU(u.ten_don_vi)] = u.id;
        const resolveUnitId = (text) => {
          const n = normU(text);
          if (!n) return null;
          if (unitMap[n] != null) return unitMap[n];
          for (const u of units) {
            if (normU(u.ten_don_vi).includes(n)) return u.id;
          }
          return null;
        };
        // Giai "Loai diem ban" -> "Hang kenh" qua bang dinh nghia loai_kenh_ban
        // (chi ap dung cho import danh sach kenh ban).
        let hangMap = new Map();
        if (isKenh) {
          try {
            await ensureLoaikenh(env);
            const defs = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT loai_diem_ban, hang_kenh FROM loai_kenh_ban").all()).results || [];
            for (const d of defs) {
              const k = normU(d.loai_diem_ban);
              if (k && !hangMap.has(k)) hangMap.set(k, d.hang_kenh);
            }
          } catch (e) { /* chua co bang dinh nghia -> khong map */ }
        }
        const sql =
          'INSERT INTO "' + table + '" (' + colNames.map((n) => '"' + n + '"').join(", ") +
          ", imported_by) VALUES (" + colNames.map(() => "?").join(", ") + ", ?)";
        let unmapped = 0;
        if (isKenh) {
          for (const row of rows) {
            const raw = row.loai_kenh;
            if (raw !== undefined && raw !== null && String(raw).trim() !== "" && !hangMap.has(normU(raw))) unmapped++;
          }
        }
        let inserted = 0;
        const CHUNK = 100;
        for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += CHUNK) {
          const batch = rows.slice(i, i + CHUNK).map((row) => {
            const vals = colNames.map((n) => {
              if (n === "don_vi_id") {
                return resolveUnitId(row.don_vi || row.quan_huyen);
              }
              if (n === "hang_kenh") {
                const raw = row.loai_kenh;
                if (raw === undefined || raw === null || String(raw).trim() === "") return null;
                return hangMap.get(normU(raw)) || null;
              }
              const v = row[n];
              return v === undefined || v === null ? null : String(v).trim();
            });
            vals.push(me.username);
            return env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...vals);
          });
          await env.DB.batch(batch);
          inserted += batch.length;
        }
        return json({ ok: true, table, inserted, mode, unmapped, skipped_empty_id: skippedEmptyId });
      }

      // ---- Cau hinh loai kenh ban (module 5.7, chi admin) ----
      // Bang dinh nghia: Loai diem ban -> Hang kenh. He thong tra bang nay
      // khi import danh sach kenh ban de xac dinh hang kenh tung diem ban.
      if (path === "/api/loai-kenh-ban" && method === "GET") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc xem cau hinh loai kenh ban", 403);
        await ensureLoaikenh(env);
        const res = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, loai_diem_ban, hang_kenh FROM loai_kenh_ban ORDER BY loai_diem_ban").all();
        return json({ ok: true, rows: res.results || [] });
      }
      if (path === "/api/loai-kenh-ban" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc sua cau hinh loai kenh ban", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const id = Number(b.id) || 0;
        const loai = String(b.loai_diem_ban || "").trim();
        const hang = String(b.hang_kenh || "").trim();
        if (!loai || !hang) return err("Nhap du Loai diem ban va Hang kenh", 422);
        await ensureLoaikenh(env);
        const all = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, loai_diem_ban FROM loai_kenh_ban").all()).results || [];
        const nk = normKbKey(loai);
        const dup = all.find((r) => r.id !== id && r.loai_diem_ban !== loai && normKbKey(r.loai_diem_ban) === nk);
        if (dup) return err("Da co dinh nghia tuong tu cho: " + dup.loai_diem_ban, 409);
        if (id) {
          const ex = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id FROM loai_kenh_ban WHERE id = ?").bind(id).first();
          if (!ex) return err("Dinh nghia khong ton tai", 404);
          await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE loai_kenh_ban SET loai_diem_ban = ?, hang_kenh = ?, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id = ?").bind(loai, hang, id).run();
          return json({ ok: true, id });
        }
        const r = await env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO loai_kenh_ban (loai_diem_ban, hang_kenh) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(loai_diem_ban) DO UPDATE SET hang_kenh = excluded.hang_kenh, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP").bind(loai, hang).run();
        return json({ ok: true, id: (r.meta && r.meta.last_row_id) || 0 });
      }
      if (path === "/api/loai-kenh-ban" && method === "DELETE") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc xoa cau hinh loai kenh ban", 403);
        const id = Number(new URL(request.url).searchParams.get("id")) || 0;
        if (!id) return err("Thieu id", 422);
        await ensureLoaikenh(env);
        await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM loai_kenh_ban WHERE id = ?").bind(id).run();
        return json({ ok: true });
      }
      if (path === "/api/loai-kenh-ban/ap-dung" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc ap dung hang kenh", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const thang = Number(b.thang), nam = Number(b.nam);
        if (!thang || thang < 1 || thang > 12 || !nam) return err("Thang/nam khong hop le", 422);
        const table = KENH_BAN_PREFIX + "_" + String(thang).padStart(2, "0") + nam;
        const ex = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name = ?").bind(table).first();
        if (!ex) return err("Chua co bang danh sach kenh ban thang " + thang + "/" + nam, 404);
        await ensureLoaikenh(env);
        await ensureListColumns(env, table, KENH_BAN_COLS);
        const defs = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT loai_diem_ban, hang_kenh FROM loai_kenh_ban").all()).results || [];
        const hangMap2 = new Map();
        for (const d of defs) {
          const k = normKbKey(d.loai_diem_ban);
          if (k && !hangMap2.has(k)) hangMap2.set(k, d.hang_kenh);
        }
        const rows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, loai_kenh FROM "' + table + '"').all()).results || [];
        let mapped = 0, unmapped = 0;
        const batch = [];
        for (const r of rows) {
          const raw = r.loai_kenh;
          if (raw === undefined || raw === null || String(raw).trim() === "") continue;
          const h = hangMap2.get(normKbKey(raw));
          if (!h) { unmapped++; continue; }
          batch.push(env.DB.prepare('UPDATE "' + table + '" SET hang_kenh = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(h, r.id));
          mapped++;
        }
        if (batch.length) await env.DB.batch(batch);
        return json({ ok: true, table, total: rows.length, mapped, unmapped });
      }

      // ---- Dong bo kenh ban tu bang danh sach kenh ban da import ----
      // Admin chon 1 bang kenh_ban_MMYYYY; dong bo THAY THE toan bo danh sach
      // diem ban (nguoi_dung role='user'): co trong bang moi -> tao moi/cap nhat;
      // ma kenh khong co trong bang moi -> xoa tai khoan.
      // dry_run=true: chi tra ke hoach (tao/cap nhat/xoa/bo qua), khong ghi.
      if (path === "/api/kenh-ban/sync" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc dong bo kenh ban", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const thang = Number(b.thang), nam = Number(b.nam);
        const dryRun = b.dry_run === true || b.dry_run === "true";
        if (!thang || thang < 1 || thang > 12 || !nam) return err("Thang/nam khong hop le", 422);
        const table = KENH_BAN_PREFIX + "_" + String(thang).padStart(2, "0") + nam;
        const ex = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name = ?").bind(table).first();
        if (!ex) return err("Chua co bang danh sach kenh ban thang " + thang + "/" + nam, 404);
        const kbRows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM "' + table + '"').all()).results || [];
        const users = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM nguoi_dung WHERE role = 'user'").all()).results || [];
        const usedUsernames = new Set((await env.DB.prepare("SELECT username FROM nguoi_dung").all()).results.map((x) => String(x.username || "").trim()).filter(Boolean));
        const trim = (v) => String(v == null ? "" : v).trim();
        const usersByCode = new Map();
        for (const u of users) {
          const c = trim(u.ma_kenh);
          if (c && !usersByCode.has(c)) usersByCode.set(c, u);
        }
        const matchedIds = new Set();
        const plannedNames = new Set();
        const plan = [];
        let skipped = 0;
        for (const r of kbRows) {
          // Khop tai khoan CHI theo ma kenh. Ma kenh lay tu cot ID diem ban
          // HRM (id_hrm) truoc, roi moi den Ma diem ban (ma_kenh) va IDDAILY;
          // khi khop van thu ca 3 cot (de tai khoan da co khong bi xoa oan).
          // So Eload chi la truong du lieu duoc dong bo theo, khong dung de
          // nhan dien diem ban.
          const codes = [r.id_hrm, r.ma_kenh, r.iddaily].map(trim).filter(Boolean);
          let u = null;
          for (const c of codes) { if (usersByCode.has(c)) { u = usersByCode.get(c); break; } }
          if (u) { matchedIds.add(u.id); plan.push({ action: "update", user: u, row: r }); }
          else if (!codes.length) skipped++;
          else if (usedUsernames.has(codes[0]) || plannedNames.has(codes[0])) skipped++;
          else { plannedNames.add(codes[0]); plan.push({ action: "create", row: r }); }
        }
        const toDelete = users.filter((u) => !matchedIds.has(u.id));
        const createdSample = plan.filter((p) => p.action === "create").slice(0, 5).map((p) => trim(p.row.ten_diem_ban) || trim(p.row.ma_kenh) || trim(p.row.id_hrm) || trim(p.row.iddaily));
        const deletedSample = toDelete.slice(0, 5).map((u) => (trim(u.ho_ten) || trim(u.username)) + (trim(u.ma_kenh) ? " (" + trim(u.ma_kenh) + ")" : ""));
        const createdN = plan.filter((p) => p.action === "create").length;
        const updatedN = plan.filter((p) => p.action === "update").length;
        if (dryRun) {
          return json({ ok: true, table, dry_run: true, total_rows: kbRows.length, total_users: users.length,
            created: createdN, updated: updatedN, deleted: toDelete.length, skipped,
            created_sample: createdSample, deleted_sample: deletedSample });
        }
        // --- Ap dung ---
        let created = 0, updated = 0, deleted = 0;
        for (const p of plan) {
          if (p.action === "update") {
            const u = p.user, r = p.row;
            const rawLoai = trim(r.hang_kenh) || trim(r.loai_kenh);
            const latP = parseCoordField(r.lat, -90, 90);
            const lngP = parseCoordField(r.lng, -180, 180);
            await env.DB.prepare(
              "UPDATE nguoi_dung SET ho_ten = ?, don_vi_id = ?, loai_kenh = ?, so_eload = ?, lat = ?, lng = ? WHERE id = ?"
            ).bind(
              trim(r.ten_diem_ban) || u.ho_ten,
              (r.don_vi_id !== null && r.don_vi_id !== undefined && r.don_vi_id !== "") ? Number(r.don_vi_id) : u.don_vi_id,
              rawLoai ? (normLoaiKenh(rawLoai) || rawLoai) : u.loai_kenh,
              trim(r.so_eload) || u.so_eload,
              (!latP.error && latP.val !== null) ? latP.val : u.lat,
              (!lngP.error && lngP.val !== null) ? lngP.val : u.lng,
              u.id
            ).run();
            updated++;
          } else {
            const r = p.row;
            const code = [r.id_hrm, r.ma_kenh, r.iddaily].map(trim).filter(Boolean)[0];
            const salt = randomSalt();
            const hash = await hashPassword("Vnpt@2026", salt);
            const rawLoai = trim(r.hang_kenh) || trim(r.loai_kenh);
            const latP = parseCoordField(r.lat, -90, 90);
            const lngP = parseCoordField(r.lng, -180, 180);
            await env.DB.prepare(
              "INSERT INTO nguoi_dung (ma_kenh, ho_ten, username, password_hash, salt, role, so_eload, so_vi, ten_nhan_vien, sdt_nhan_vien, ma_hrm, ma_nhan_vien, chuc_danh, don_vi_id, leader_id, loai_kenh, can_import, modules, lat, lng) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'user', ?, '', '', '', '', '', '', ?, NULL, ?, 0, ?, ?, ?)"
            ).bind(
              code, trim(r.ten_diem_ban) || code, code, hash, salt, trim(r.so_eload),
              (r.don_vi_id !== null && r.don_vi_id !== undefined && r.don_vi_id !== "") ? Number(r.don_vi_id) : null,
              rawLoai ? (normLoaiKenh(rawLoai) || rawLoai) : "",
              JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_MODULES),
              latP.error ? null : latP.val, lngP.error ? null : lngP.val
            ).run();
            created++;
          }
        }
        for (const u of toDelete) {
          if (u.id === me.id) { skipped++; continue; }
          const leadRef = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM nguoi_dung WHERE leader_id = ?").bind(u.id).first();
          if (leadRef && leadRef.c > 0) { skipped++; continue; }
          await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM nguoi_dung WHERE id = ?").bind(u.id).run();
          deleted++;
        }
        return json({ ok: true, table, created, updated, deleted, skipped, total_rows: kbRows.length, total_users: users.length });
      }

      // ---- Bao cao thong ke ----
      // Danh sach diem ban cho bo loc "Theo diem ban" trong Bao cao thong ke.
      // Admin/subadmin: toan bo diem ban; lanh dao (va chuyen vien tuong
      // duong): diem ban trong cay don vi cua minh; con lai: chi chinh minh.
      if (path === "/api/pos-list" && method === "GET") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "reports")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen xem bao cao", 403);
        let sql, binds;
        if (isPrivileged) {
          sql = "SELECT u.id, u.ma_kenh, u.ho_ten, u.username, u.don_vi_id, d.ten_don_vi AS don_vi_ten FROM nguoi_dung u LEFT JOIN don_vi d ON d.id = u.don_vi_id WHERE u.role = 'user' ORDER BY u.ho_ten";
          binds = [];
        } else if (isLeaderLike && me.don_vi_id) {
          sql = unitSubtreeCTE() + " SELECT u.id, u.ma_kenh, u.ho_ten, u.username, u.don_vi_id, d.ten_don_vi AS don_vi_ten FROM nguoi_dung u LEFT JOIN don_vi d ON d.id = u.don_vi_id WHERE u.role = 'user' AND u.don_vi_id IN (SELECT id FROM subtree) ORDER BY u.ho_ten";
          binds = [me.don_vi_id];
        } else {
          sql = "SELECT u.id, u.ma_kenh, u.ho_ten, u.username, u.don_vi_id, d.ten_don_vi AS don_vi_ten FROM nguoi_dung u LEFT JOIN don_vi d ON d.id = u.don_vi_id WHERE u.id = ?";
          binds = [me.id];
        }
        const res = await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...binds).all();
        return json({ ok: true, list: res.results || [] });
      }
      if (path === "/api/reports/summary" && method === "GET") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "reports") && !hasModule(me, "dashboard")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen xem bao cao", 403);
        const from = url.searchParams.get("from") || "0000-01-01";
        const to = url.searchParams.get("to") || "9999-12-31";
        const group = url.searchParams.get("group") || "day";
        const loai = (url.searchParams.get("loai") || "ALL").toUpperCase();
        const statusFilter = parseStatusFilter(url.searchParams.get("trang_thai"));
        const r = await resolveScopeUnit(env, me, isLeaderLike, isPrivileged, url.searchParams.get("don_vi"));
        if (!r.ok) return err(r.msg, r.code);
        const pr = await resolvePosUser(env, me, isPrivileged, isLeaderLike, url.searchParams.get("diem_ban"));
        if (!pr.ok) return err(pr.msg, pr.code);
        const d = await aggregateSummary(env, me, isPrivileged, isUnitScoped, { from, to, group, loai, scopeUnit: r.unit, statusFilter, posUser: pr.pos });
        return json({ ok: true, ...d, pos: pr.pos ? { id: pr.pos.id, ho_ten: pr.pos.ho_ten, username: pr.pos.username } : null });
      }
      // Tong hop theo don vi: moi dong la tong so lieu cua cac diem ban thuoc
      // don vi do (map qua so_eload/so_vi/ma_kenh/ma_hrm-sdt). Admin/subadmin
      // thay toan bo don vi; lanh dao chi thay cac don vi trong cay cua minh.
      if (path === "/api/reports/by-unit" && method === "GET") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "reports")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen xem bao cao", 403);
        if (!isPrivileged && !isLeaderLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin/lanh dao moi xem duoc bao cao theo don vi", 403);
        const from = url.searchParams.get("from") || "0000-01-01";
        const to = url.searchParams.get("to") || "9999-12-31";
        const loai = (url.searchParams.get("loai") || "ALL").toUpperCase();
        const statusFilter = parseStatusFilter(url.searchParams.get("trang_thai"));
        const pr = await resolvePosUser(env, me, isPrivileged, isLeaderLike, url.searchParams.get("diem_ban"));
        if (!pr.ok) return err(pr.msg, pr.code);
        const posUser = pr.pos;
        let units;
        if (posUser && posUser.don_vi_id) {
          // Loc theo diem ban: chi hien dong don vi cua diem ban do
          units = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, ma_don_vi, ten_don_vi, parent_id FROM don_vi WHERE id = ?").bind(posUser.don_vi_id).all()).results || [];
        } else if (isPrivileged) {
          units = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, ma_don_vi, ten_don_vi, parent_id FROM don_vi ORDER BY ten_don_vi").all()).results || [];
        } else {
          units = (await env.DB.prepare(unitSubtreeCTE() + " SELECT d.id, d.ma_don_vi, d.ten_don_vi, d.parent_id FROM don_vi d WHERE d.id IN (SELECT id FROM subtree) ORDER BY d.ten_don_vi").bind(me.don_vi_id).all()).results || [];
        }
        const out = [];
        for (const u of units) {
          const d = await aggregateSummary(env, me, isPrivileged, isUnitScoped, { from, to, group: "month", loai, scopeUnit: u.id, statusFilter, posUser });
          out.push({ don_vi_id: u.id, ma_don_vi: u.ma_don_vi, ten_don_vi: u.ten_don_vi, so_giao_dich: d.tong_giao_dich, tong_tien: d.tong_tien });
        }
        return json({ ok: true, units: out, pos: posUser ? { id: posUser.id, ho_ten: posUser.ho_ten, username: posUser.username } : null });
      }
      // Dashboard ban hang: tong hop phat trien thue bao di dong + doanh thu ban
      // goi di dong phat trien moi (truoc thue = gia goi / 1,1) theo don vi va
      // theo diem ban. Loai du lieu do admin thiet ke trong module Cai dat
      // (map 5 cot sales_*: ten don vi, so eload, ma kenh, gia goi, so luong).
      // NGUYEN TAC MAP: theo don vi -> can cu ten don vi trong du lieu; theo
      // diem ban -> can cu ma kenh hoac so eload; chi tinh cac thue bao do
      // tai khoan Eload cua diem ban (role='user') phat trien.
      // Ke hoach kinh doanh theo thang + don vi: PSSL / san luong thue bao / doanh thu.
      // don_vi_id = 0 la muc "Toan tinh" (tuong thich du lieu ke hoach PSSL cu).
      const KEHOACH_DDL = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ke_hoach (thang TEXT NOT NULL, don_vi_id INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, loai TEXT NOT NULL, so_luong REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, updated_by TEXT, updated_at TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (thang, don_vi_id, loai))";
      async function ensureKeHoach(env) {
        await env.DB.prepare(KEHOACH_DDL).run();
        try {
          const old = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='ke_hoach_pssl'").first();
          if (old) {
            await env.DB.prepare("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO ke_hoach (thang, don_vi_id, loai, so_luong, updated_by, updated_at) SELECT thang, 0, 'pssl', so_luong, updated_by, updated_at FROM ke_hoach_pssl").run();
            await env.DB.prepare("DROP TABLE ke_hoach_pssl").run();
          }
        } catch (e) { /* bo qua */ }
      }
      const KEHOACH_LOAI = ["pssl", "subs", "revenue"];
      if (path === "/api/ke-hoach" && method === "GET") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "salesdash") && !hasModule(me, "import")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen xem ke hoach", 403);
        const thang = String(url.searchParams.get("thang") || "").trim();
        const loai = String(url.searchParams.get("loai") || "").trim();
        if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(thang)) return err("Thang khong hop le (YYYY-MM)", 422);
        if (loai && !KEHOACH_LOAI.includes(loai)) return err("Loai ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        await ensureKeHoach(env);
        const binds = loai ? [thang, loai] : [thang];
        const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT don_vi_id, loai, so_luong FROM ke_hoach WHERE thang = ?" + (loai ? " AND loai = ?" : "")).bind(...binds).all();
        return json({ ok: true, thang, plans: r.results || [] });
      }
      if (path === "/api/ke-hoach" && method === "PUT") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc giao ke hoach", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const thang = String(b.thang || "").trim();
        const loai = String(b.loai || "").trim();
        const items = Array.isArray(b.items) ? b.items : [];
        if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(thang)) return err("Thang khong hop le (YYYY-MM)", 422);
        if (!KEHOACH_LOAI.includes(loai)) return err("Loai ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        await ensureKeHoach(env);
        const stmt = env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO ke_hoach (thang, don_vi_id, loai, so_luong, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(thang, don_vi_id, loai) DO UPDATE SET so_luong = excluded.so_luong, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at");
        const batch = items.map((it) => stmt.bind(thang, Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(it.don_vi_id) || 0)), loai, Math.max(0, Number(it.so_luong) || 0), me.username || "", saigonNow()));
        if (batch.length) await env.DB.batch(batch);
        return json({ ok: true, thang, loai, saved: batch.length });
      }
      // Parse so lieu ke hoach: number tu Excel dung truc tiep; chuoi thi hieu
      // dinh dang Viet ("16.190.000" -> 16190000, "16,19" -> 16.19).
      // LUU Y: khong duoc xoa dau cham cua number (VD 130800000.00000001 la
      // dau thap phan, xoa di se nhan gia tri len hang nghin lan).
      const parsePlanNum = (v) => {
        if (typeof v === "number") return v;
        const s = String(v == null ? "" : v).trim().replace(/\s+/g, "");
        if (!s) return NaN;
        if (s.includes(",")) return Number(s.replace(/\./g, "").replace(",", "."));
        if (s.includes(".")) {
          const parts = s.split(".");
          if (parts.length > 1 && parts.slice(1).every((q) => /^\d{3}$/.test(q))) return Number(parts.join(""));
          return Number(s);
        }
        return Number(s);
      };
      if (path === "/api/ke-hoach/import" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc import ke hoach", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const nam = parseInt(b.nam, 10);
        const loai = String(b.loai || "").trim();
        const mode = ["create", "update", "replace"].includes(b.mode) ? b.mode : "create";
        const rows = Array.isArray(b.rows) ? b.rows : [];
        if (!nam || nam < 2000 || nam > 2100) return err("Nam ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        if (!KEHOACH_LOAI.includes(loai)) return err("Loai ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        await ensureKeHoach(env);
        const normU2 = (s) => String(s == null ? "" : s).trim().toLowerCase()
          .normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").replace(/đ/g, "d")
          .replace(/[–—−‐‒]/g, "-").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/\s*-\s*/g, "-");
        const allUnits = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT id, ten_don_vi FROM don_vi").all()).results || [];
        const nameToId = {};
        for (const u of allUnits) nameToId[normU2(u.ten_don_vi)] = u.id;
        if (mode === "replace") {
          await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM ke_hoach WHERE substr(thang, 1, 4) = ? AND loai = ?").bind(String(nam), loai).run();
        }
        const exRows = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT thang, don_vi_id FROM ke_hoach WHERE substr(thang, 1, 4) = ? AND loai = ?").bind(String(nam), loai).all()).results || [];
        const existed = new Set(exRows.map((r) => r.thang + "|" + r.don_vi_id));
        let created = 0, updated = 0, skipped = 0;
        const errors = [];
        const up = env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO ke_hoach (thang, don_vi_id, loai, so_luong, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(thang, don_vi_id, loai) DO UPDATE SET so_luong = excluded.so_luong, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at");
        const batch = [];
        const nowS2 = saigonNow(), by = me.username || "";
        for (const r of rows) {
          const uname = String(r.don_vi == null ? "" : r.don_vi).trim();
          if (!uname) continue;
          const nu = normU2(uname);
          let duid = null;
          if (nu === "toan tinh") duid = 0;
          else if (nu === "chua xac dinh") { skipped++; continue; }
          else if (nameToId[nu] != null) duid = nameToId[nu];
          else { errors.push({ don_vi: uname, reason: "Không tìm thấy đơn vị" }); continue; }
          const months = Array.isArray(r.months) ? r.months : [];
          for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
            const v = months[m - 1];
            if (v === "" || v == null) continue;
            const num = parsePlanNum(v);
            if (!isFinite(num) || num < 0) { errors.push({ don_vi: uname, reason: "Tháng " + m + ": giá trị không hợp lệ" }); continue; }
            const thang = nam + "-" + String(m).padStart(2, "0");
            const key = thang + "|" + duid;
            if (mode === "create" && existed.has(key)) { skipped++; continue; }
            if (existed.has(key)) updated++; else { created++; existed.add(key); }
            batch.push(up.bind(thang, duid, loai, num, by, nowS2));
          }
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < batch.length; i += 40) {
          const chunk = batch.slice(i, i + 40);
          if (chunk.length) await env.DB.batch(chunk);
        }
        return json({ ok: true, nam, loai, mode, created, updated, skipped, errors: errors.slice(0, 50) });
      }
      // ---- Ke hoach TTCSKH (Dashboard TTCSKH) ----
      // Bang ke_hoach_ttcskh: ke hoach doanh thu / thue bao theo thang, cho tap the
      // (loai='tap_the', doi_tuong='TOAN TTCSKH' hoac ten to/nhom) va ca nhan
      // (loai='ca_nhan', doi_tuong=ten dien thoai vien), theo 4 chuong trinh OB:
      // CKN (gia han chu ky ngan), CKD (gia han chu ky dai), BG (ban goi),
      // HVC (gia han/ban goi tap thue bao HVC).
      const KHTTCSKH_DDL = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ke_hoach_ttcskh (thang TEXT NOT NULL, loai TEXT NOT NULL, doi_tuong TEXT NOT NULL, chuong_trinh TEXT NOT NULL, chi_tieu TEXT NOT NULL, so_luong REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, updated_by TEXT, updated_at TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (thang, loai, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu))";
      async function ensureKhTtcskh(env) { await env.DB.prepare(KHTTCSKH_DDL).run(); }
      const KHTTCSKH_CT = ["CKN", "CKD", "BG", "HVC"];
      const KHTTCSKH_CHITIEU = ["doanh_thu", "thue_bao"];
      const KHTTCSKH_LOAI = ["tap_the", "ca_nhan"];
      if (path === "/api/ke-hoach-ttcskh" && method === "GET") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc xem ke hoach TTCSKH", 403);
        const nam = String(url.searchParams.get("nam") || "").trim();
        const loai = String(url.searchParams.get("loai") || "").trim();
        const chuong_trinh = String(url.searchParams.get("chuong_trinh") || "").trim();
        const chi_tieu = String(url.searchParams.get("chi_tieu") || "").trim();
        if (nam && !/^\d{4}$/.test(nam)) return err("Nam khong hop le", 422);
        if (loai && !KHTTCSKH_LOAI.includes(loai)) return err("Loai ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        if (chuong_trinh && !KHTTCSKH_CT.includes(chuong_trinh)) return err("Chuong trinh khong hop le", 422);
        if (chi_tieu && !KHTTCSKH_CHITIEU.includes(chi_tieu)) return err("Chi tieu khong hop le", 422);
        await ensureKhTtcskh(env);
        const conds = [], binds = [];
        if (nam) { conds.push("substr(thang, 1, 4) = ?"); binds.push(nam); }
        if (loai) { conds.push("loai = ?"); binds.push(loai); }
        if (chuong_trinh) { conds.push("chuong_trinh = ?"); binds.push(chuong_trinh); }
        if (chi_tieu) { conds.push("chi_tieu = ?"); binds.push(chi_tieu); }
        const r = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT thang, loai, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu, so_luong FROM ke_hoach_ttcskh" + (conds.length ? " WHERE " + conds.join(" AND ") : "") + " ORDER BY thang, loai, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu").bind(...binds).all();
        return json({ ok: true, plans: r.results || [] });
      }
      if (path === "/api/ke-hoach-ttcskh" && method === "PUT") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc giao ke hoach TTCSKH", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const thang = String(b.thang || "").trim();
        const loai = String(b.loai || "").trim();
        const items = Array.isArray(b.items) ? b.items : [];
        if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(thang)) return err("Thang khong hop le (YYYY-MM)", 422);
        if (!KHTTCSKH_LOAI.includes(loai)) return err("Loai ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        await ensureKhTtcskh(env);
        const stmt = env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO ke_hoach_ttcskh (thang, loai, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu, so_luong, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(thang, loai, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu) DO UPDATE SET so_luong = excluded.so_luong, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at");
        const batch = [];
        for (const it of items) {
          const dt = String(it.doi_tuong || "").trim();
          const ct = String(it.chuong_trinh || "").trim();
          const ctieu = String(it.chi_tieu || "").trim();
          if (!dt || !KHTTCSKH_CT.includes(ct) || !KHTTCSKH_CHITIEU.includes(ctieu)) continue;
          batch.push(stmt.bind(thang, loai, dt, ct, ctieu, Math.max(0, Number(it.so_luong) || 0), me.username || "", saigonNow()));
        }
        if (batch.length) await env.DB.batch(batch);
        return json({ ok: true, thang, loai, saved: batch.length });
      }
      if (path === "/api/ke-hoach-ttcskh" && method === "DELETE") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc xoa ke hoach TTCSKH", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const thang = String(b.thang || "").trim();
        const loai = String(b.loai || "").trim();
        const dt = String(b.doi_tuong || "").trim();
        const ct = String(b.chuong_trinh || "").trim();
        const ctieu = String(b.chi_tieu || "").trim();
        if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(thang) || !KHTTCSKH_LOAI.includes(loai) || !dt || !KHTTCSKH_CT.includes(ct) || !KHTTCSKH_CHITIEU.includes(ctieu)) return err("Thong tin xoa ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        await ensureKhTtcskh(env);
        await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM ke_hoach_ttcskh WHERE thang = ? AND loai = ? AND doi_tuong = ? AND chuong_trinh = ? AND chi_tieu = ?").bind(thang, loai, dt, ct, ctieu).run();
        return json({ ok: true });
      }
      if (path === "/api/ke-hoach-ttcskh/import" && method === "POST") {
        if (!isAdminLike) return err("Chi admin/subadmin moi duoc import ke hoach TTCSKH", 403);
        const b = await readBody(request);
        const nam = parseInt(b.nam, 10);
        const loai = String(b.loai || "").trim();
        const chuong_trinh = String(b.chuong_trinh || "").trim();
        const chi_tieu = String(b.chi_tieu || "").trim();
        const mode = ["create", "update", "replace"].includes(b.mode) ? b.mode : "create";
        const rows = Array.isArray(b.rows) ? b.rows : [];
        if (!nam || nam < 2000 || nam > 2100) return err("Nam ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        if (!KHTTCSKH_LOAI.includes(loai)) return err("Loai ke hoach khong hop le", 422);
        if (!KHTTCSKH_CT.includes(chuong_trinh)) return err("Chuong trinh khong hop le", 422);
        if (!KHTTCSKH_CHITIEU.includes(chi_tieu)) return err("Chi tieu khong hop le", 422);
        await ensureKhTtcskh(env);
        if (mode === "replace") {
          await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM ke_hoach_ttcskh WHERE substr(thang, 1, 4) = ? AND loai = ? AND chuong_trinh = ? AND chi_tieu = ?").bind(String(nam), loai, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu).run();
        }
        const exRows = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT thang, doi_tuong FROM ke_hoach_ttcskh WHERE substr(thang, 1, 4) = ? AND loai = ? AND chuong_trinh = ? AND chi_tieu = ?").bind(String(nam), loai, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu).all()).results || [];
        const existed = new Set(exRows.map((r) => r.thang + "|" + r.doi_tuong));
        let created = 0, updated = 0, skipped = 0;
        const errors = [];
        const up = env.DB.prepare("INSERT INTO ke_hoach_ttcskh (thang, loai, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu, so_luong, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(thang, loai, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu) DO UPDATE SET so_luong = excluded.so_luong, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at");
        const batch = [];
        const nowS3 = saigonNow(), by3 = me.username || "";
        for (const r of rows) {
          const dt = String(r.doi_tuong == null ? "" : r.doi_tuong).trim();
          if (!dt) continue;
          const months = Array.isArray(r.months) ? r.months : [];
          for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
            const v = months[m - 1];
            if (v === "" || v == null) continue;
            const num = parsePlanNum(v);
            if (!isFinite(num) || num < 0) { errors.push({ doi_tuong: dt, reason: "Tháng " + m + ": giá trị không hợp lệ" }); continue; }
            const thang = nam + "-" + String(m).padStart(2, "0");
            const key = thang + "|" + dt;
            if (mode === "create" && existed.has(key)) { skipped++; continue; }
            if (existed.has(key)) updated++; else { created++; existed.add(key); }
            batch.push(up.bind(thang, loai, dt, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu, num, by3, nowS3));
          }
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < batch.length; i += 40) {
          const chunk = batch.slice(i, i + 40);
          if (chunk.length) await env.DB.batch(chunk);
        }
        return json({ ok: true, nam, loai, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu, mode, created, updated, skipped, errors: errors.slice(0, 50) });
      }
      // ---- Dashboard TTCSKH ----
      // KHUNG TONG HOP (11/2026): cau truc phan hoi day du theo yeu cau — tap the
      // (4 chuong trinh CKN/CKD/BG/HVC + bo chi so tien trinh) va ca nhan (theo
      // chuong trinh / theo hinh thuc OB / chat luong cuoc goi). So lieu THUC HIEN
      // tam = 0 vi nguon du lieu OB (cuoc goi, gia han/ban goi) chua duoc dinh nghia
      // — anh se mo ta cau truc du lieu sau; luc do noi phan tong hop vao day.
      // Ke hoach TTCSKH (bang ke_hoach_ttcskh) thi doc that ngay tu bay gio.
      if (path === "/api/mobile-overview" && method === "GET") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "mobiledash")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen xem Dashboard TTCSKH", 403);
        const ky = url.searchParams.get("ky") === "nam" ? "nam" : "thang";
        const nowM = saigonNow();
        let thang = String(url.searchParams.get("thang") || "").trim();
        if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(thang)) thang = nowM.slice(0, 7);
        let nam = parseInt(url.searchParams.get("nam"), 10);
        if (!nam || nam < 2000 || nam > 2100) nam = Number(nowM.slice(0, 4));
        const months = [];
        let curMonths, prevMonths, trendMonths, periodLabel;
        if (ky === "nam") {
          for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) months.push(nam + "-" + String(m).padStart(2, "0"));
          curMonths = months.slice(); prevMonths = months.map((mk) => (nam - 1) + mk.slice(4));
          // Bieu do doanh thu: tu dau nam den thang hien tai — nam hien tai thi
          // cat cac thang tuong lai, nam da qua thi du 12 thang (14/11)
          trendMonths = Number(nam) === Number(nowM.slice(0, 4)) ? curMonths.filter((mk) => Number(mk.slice(5, 7)) <= Number(nowM.slice(5, 7))) : curMonths;
          periodLabel = "năm " + nam;
        } else {
          let y = Number(thang.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(thang.slice(5, 7));
          for (let i = 11; i >= 0; i--) { let mm = m - i, yy = y; while (mm <= 0) { mm += 12; yy--; } months.push(yy + "-" + String(mm).padStart(2, "0")); }
          curMonths = [months[11]]; prevMonths = [months[10]];
          // Bieu do doanh thu: tu dau nam den thang T — khong lay thang 12 nam truoc (14/11)
          trendMonths = [];
          for (let mm = 1; mm <= m; mm++) trendMonths.push(y + "-" + String(mm).padStart(2, "0"));
          periodLabel = "tháng " + Number(thang.slice(5, 7)) + "/" + thang.slice(0, 4);
        }
        await ensureKhTtcskh(env);
        const planRows = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT thang, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu, so_luong FROM ke_hoach_ttcskh WHERE loai = 'tap_the'").all()).results || [];
        const planTeam = {};
        // Ke hoach doanh thu anh nhap tinh bang TRIEU DONG (14/11) — nhan 1e6
        // de so sanh voi doanh thu thuc hien (dong). Chi tieu thue_bao giu nguyen.
        for (const r of planRows) {
          const k = r.thang + "|" + r.chuong_trinh + "|" + r.chi_tieu;
          planTeam[k] = (planTeam[k] || 0) + (Number(r.so_luong) || 0) * (r.chi_tieu === "doanh_thu" ? 1000000 : 1);
        }
        const planGet = (mk, ct, chi) => planTeam[mk + "|" + ct + "|" + chi] || 0;
        const planSum = (mks, ct, chi) => mks.reduce((a, mk) => a + planGet(mk, ct, chi), 0);
        const personRows = (await env.DB.prepare("SELECT thang, doi_tuong, chuong_trinh, chi_tieu, so_luong FROM ke_hoach_ttcskh WHERE loai = 'ca_nhan'").all()).results || [];
        const personPlan = {};
        const personSet = new Set();
        for (const r of personRows) {
          if (!curMonths.includes(r.thang)) continue;
          personSet.add(r.doi_tuong);
          const k = r.doi_tuong + "|" + r.chuong_trinh + "|" + r.chi_tieu;
          personPlan[k] = (personPlan[k] || 0) + (Number(r.so_luong) || 0) * (r.chi_tieu === "doanh_thu" ? 1000000 : 1);
        }
        const PROGS = [
          { code: "CKN", name: "Gia hạn gói chu kỳ ngắn" },
          { code: "CKD", name: "Gia hạn gói chu kỳ dài" },
          { code: "BG", name: "Bán gói" },
          { code: "HVC", name: "Gia hạn/bán gói tập thuê bao HVC" },
        ];
        const pct = (a, b) => (b > 0 ? Math.round((a / b) * 1000) / 10 : null);
        // ---- Tong hop so lieu that cho Dashboard TTCSKH (tap the) ----
        // Nguon (theo dac ta anh gui 14/11):
        // - OBCKN/OBCKD/OBBG/OBHVC_MMYYYY: doanh thu = SUM(cot doanh thu) / 1,1
        //   (truoc thue; lam tron sau khi chia), thue bao = COUNT(so_thue_bao).
        // - THCKN/THCKD/THBG/THHVC_MMYYYY: chi so tien trinh + ty le chuyen doi
        //   (ty le chuyen doi lay so luu san trong cot ty le cua file TH).
        // - IPCC_MMYYYY: binh quan so lan goi = tong_cuoc_goi_ra /
        //   tong_tb_can_thuc_hien theo tung chuong trinh (loc theo cot
        //   chuong_trinh_ob).
        // HVC dung ten cot rieng trong bang TH (THHVC khong co cac cot sl_tb_*
        // tuong ung): tong_tb_da_goi / tong_tb_dua_vao_ob_hvc /
        // tong_sl_tb_ket_noi_tc.
        const MOB_SRC = {
          CKN: { ob: "OBCKN", rev: "doanh_thu_dk_gh", th: "THCKN", called: "sl_tb_da_thuc_hien_ob", total: "sl_tb_dua_vao_ob", conn: "sl_tb_ket_noi_tc", conv: "ty_le_tb_gia_han_goi" },
          CKD: { ob: "OBCKD", rev: "doanh_thu_dk_gh", th: "THCKD", called: "sl_tb_da_thuc_hien_ob", total: "sl_tb_dua_vao_ob", conn: "sl_tb_ket_noi_tc", conv: "ty_le_tb_gia_han_goi" },
          BG: { ob: "OBBG", rev: "doanh_thu_dang_ky", th: "THBG", called: "sl_tb_da_thuc_hien_ob", total: "sl_tb_dua_vao_ob", conn: "sl_tb_ket_noi_tc", conv: "ty_le_tb_dang_ky_goi" },
          HVC: { ob: "OBHVC", rev: "doanh_thu_dk_gh", th: "THHVC", called: "tong_tb_da_goi", total: "tong_tb_dua_vao_ob_hvc", conn: "tong_sl_tb_ket_noi_tc", conv: "ty_le_tb_dk_gh_tong_tap" },
        };
        const tableExists = async (name) => !!(await env.DB.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name = ?").bind(name).first());
        const normProg = (v) => String(v || "").normalize("NFD").replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "").replace(/đ/g, "d").replace(/Đ/g, "D").toLowerCase();
        const obCache = new Map();
        const obMonth = async (code, mk) => {
          const key = code + "|" + mk;
          if (obCache.has(key)) return obCache.get(key);
          const cfg = MOB_SRC[code];
          const out = { subs: 0, rev: 0, exists: false };
          const table = monthTableName(cfg.ob, mk);
          if (table && (await tableExists(table))) {
            const r = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(so_thue_bao) AS subs, COALESCE(SUM("' + cfg.rev + '"), 0) AS rev FROM "' + table + '"').first();
            out.subs = Number(r?.subs) || 0;
            out.rev = Math.round((Number(r?.rev) || 0) / 1.1); // doanh thu truoc thue
            out.exists = true;
          }
          obCache.set(key, out);
          return out;
        };
        const obSum = async (code, mks) => {
          let subs = 0, rev = 0, exists = false;
          for (const mk of mks) { const o = await obMonth(code, mk); subs += o.subs; rev += o.rev; exists = exists || o.exists; }
          return { subs, rev, exists };
        };
        // So sanh T-1 CUNG NGAY (14/11): neu ky dang xem chua ket thuc (thang T
        // la thang hien tai / nam Y la nam hien tai) thi ky truoc chi tinh den
        // cung ngay trong thang — VD: hom nay 14/11 thi T-1 chi tinh den 14/10.
        // Ky da ket thuc thi lay tron ky truoc. Ngay lay tu cot ngay_mo_dv
        // (co o ca 4 bang OB); dong khong doc duoc ngay thi bo qua.
        const nowY = Number(nowM.slice(0, 4)), curMo = Number(nowM.slice(5, 7)), nowD = Number(nowM.slice(8, 10));
        const isCurrentPeriod = ky === "nam" ? Number(nam) === nowY : thang === nowM.slice(0, 7);
        const prevCutFor = (mk) => {
          if (!isCurrentPeriod) return null;
          if (ky === "thang") return nowD; // prevMonths chi co 1 thang T-1
          const mm = Number(mk.slice(5, 7));
          if (mm > curMo) return "skip"; // thang doi chieu sau thang hien tai thi bo qua
          if (mm === curMo) return nowD;
          return null;
        };
        const dayOf = (v) => {
          if (v == null) return null;
          const str = String(v).trim();
          if (!str) return null;
          let m = str.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})/); // ISO 2026-10-14
          if (m) return Number(m[3]);
          m = str.match(/^(\d{1,2})[/\-.](\d{1,2})[/\-.](\d{2,4})/); // 14/10/2026
          if (m) return Number(m[1]);
          if (/^\d{5}$/.test(str)) { // so serial Excel
            const n = Number(str);
            if (n >= 30000 && n <= 80000) return new Date(Math.round((n - 25569) * 86400 * 1000)).getUTCDate();
          }
          const d = new Date(str); // chuoi Date cua JS
          return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d.getDate();
        };
        const obMonthCut = async (code, mk, dayCut) => {
          const cfg = MOB_SRC[code];
          const table = monthTableName(cfg.ob, mk);
          const out = { subs: 0, rev: 0, exists: false };
          if (!table || !(await tableExists(table))) return out;
          out.exists = true;
          const cols = (await env.DB.prepare('PRAGMA table_info("' + table + '")').all()).results || [];
          if (!cols.some((c) => c.name === "ngay_mo_dv")) return obMonth(code, mk); // khong co cot ngay thi lay tron thang
          const rows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT so_thue_bao, "' + cfg.rev + '" AS rev, ngay_mo_dv FROM "' + table + '"').all()).results || [];
          let revSum = 0;
          for (const r of rows) {
            const d = dayOf(r.ngay_mo_dv);
            if (d == null || d > dayCut) continue;
            if (r.so_thue_bao != null) out.subs += 1;
            revSum += Number(r.rev) || 0;
          }
          out.rev = Math.round(revSum / 1.1);
          return out;
        };
        const prevSum = async (code) => {
          let subs = 0, rev = 0, exists = false;
          for (const mk of prevMonths) {
            const cut = prevCutFor(mk);
            if (cut === "skip") continue;
            const o = cut == null ? await obMonth(code, mk) : await obMonthCut(code, mk, cut);
            subs += o.subs; rev += o.rev; exists = exists || o.exists;
          }
          return { subs, rev, exists };
        };
        const prevCutoff = !isCurrentPeriod ? null : ky === "thang"
          ? { thang: prevMonths[0], ngay: nowD }
          : { thang: (nam - 1) + "-" + String(curMo).padStart(2, "0"), ngay: nowD };
        const thMonth = async (code, mk) => {
          const cfg = MOB_SRC[code];
          const out = { called: 0, total: 0, conn: 0, conv: null, exists: false };
          const table = monthTableName(cfg.th, mk);
          if (!table || !(await tableExists(table))) return out;
          out.exists = true;
          const r = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COALESCE(SUM("' + cfg.called + '"), 0) AS called, COALESCE(SUM("' + cfg.total + '"), 0) AS total, COALESCE(SUM("' + cfg.conn + '"), 0) AS conn FROM "' + table + '"').first();
          out.called = Number(r?.called) || 0;
          out.total = Number(r?.total) || 0;
          out.conn = Number(r?.conn) || 0;
          // Ty le chuyen doi: lay so luu san trong file TH (thuong 1 dong QNH).
          // Neu nhieu dong: uu tien dong tinh QNH, neu khong thi dong dau tien.
          const rows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT tinh, "' + cfg.conv + '" AS conv FROM "' + table + '"').all()).results || [];
          const pick = rows.find((x) => normProg(x.tinh).includes("qnh")) || rows[0];
          if (pick && pick.conv !== null && pick.conv !== undefined && pick.conv !== "") {
            const cv = Number(pick.conv);
            if (!Number.isNaN(cv)) out.conv = cv;
          }
          return out;
        };
        const ipccMonth = async (mk) => {
          const out = { CKN: { calls: 0, total: 0 }, CKD: { calls: 0, total: 0 }, BG: { calls: 0, total: 0 }, HVC: { calls: 0, total: 0 }, exists: false, unmatched: [] };
          const table = monthTableName("IPCC", mk);
          if (!table || !(await tableExists(table))) return out;
          out.exists = true;
          const rows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT chuong_trinh_ob AS ct, COALESCE(SUM(tong_cuoc_goi_ra), 0) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(tong_tb_can_thuc_hien), 0) AS total FROM "' + table + '" GROUP BY chuong_trinh_ob').all()).results || [];
          for (const r of rows) {
            // Chuan hoa khoang trang (gom NBSP) truoc khi nhan dien ten chuong trinh
            const n = normProg(r.ct).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
            let code = null;
            if (n.includes("hvc")) code = "HVC";
            else if (n.includes("ckn")) code = "CKN";
            else if (n.includes("ckd")) code = "CKD";
            else if (n.includes("bg") || n.includes("ban goi") || n.includes("bangoi") || n.includes("ban hang")) code = "BG"; // "Bangoi" viet lien, VD "Chiến dịch OB Bangoi_QNH" (14/11)
            if (!code) {
              const raw = r.ct == null ? "" : String(r.ct).trim();
              if (raw !== "" && !out.unmatched.includes(raw)) out.unmatched.push(raw);
              continue;
            }
            out[code].calls += Number(r.calls) || 0;
            out[code].total += Number(r.total) || 0;
          }
          return out;
        };
        // Thang cung ky nam truoc (ky=thang: T-12; ky=nam: nam truoc = prevMonths)
        let yoyMonths = prevMonths;
        if (ky === "thang") yoyMonths = [(Number(thang.slice(0, 4)) - 1) + "-" + thang.slice(5, 7)];
        const programs = [];
        let totSubs = 0, totRev = 0, totPrevRev = 0, totYoyRev = 0, hasOb = false;
        for (const p of PROGS) {
          const cur = await obSum(p.code, curMonths);
          const prev = await prevSum(p.code);
          const yoy = await obSum(p.code, yoyMonths);
          const planRev = planSum(curMonths, p.code, "doanh_thu");
          const planSubs = planSum(curMonths, p.code, "thue_bao");
          totSubs += cur.subs; totRev += cur.rev; totPrevRev += prev.rev; totYoyRev += yoy.rev;
          hasOb = hasOb || cur.exists;
          programs.push({ code: p.code, name: p.name, subs: cur.subs, revenue: cur.rev, plan_rev: planRev, plan_subs: planSubs, pct_plan_rev: pct(cur.rev, planRev), pct_plan_subs: pct(cur.subs, planSubs), rev_prev: prev.rev, rev_yoy_pct: pct(cur.rev - yoy.rev, yoy.rev), subs_prev: prev.subs });
        }
        const totalPlanRev = programs.reduce((a, p) => a + p.plan_rev, 0);
        const totalPlanSubs = programs.reduce((a, p) => a + p.plan_subs, 0);
        const trend = [];
        for (const mk of trendMonths) {
          let subs = 0, revenue = 0;
          for (const p of PROGS) { const o = await obMonth(p.code, mk); subs += o.subs; revenue += o.rev; }
          trend.push({
            month: mk, subs, revenue,
            plan_rev: PROGS.reduce((a, p) => a + planGet(mk, p.code, "doanh_thu"), 0),
            plan_subs: PROGS.reduce((a, p) => a + planGet(mk, p.code, "thue_bao"), 0),
          });
        }
        // Bo chi so tien trinh OB (ky hien tai)
        let obCalled = 0, obTotal = 0, connTotal = 0, hasTh = false;
        const obSuccess = {}, convRate = {};
        for (const p of PROGS) {
          let called = 0, total = 0, conn = 0;
          const convs = [];
          for (const mk of curMonths) {
            const t = await thMonth(p.code, mk);
            if (t.exists) hasTh = true;
            called += t.called; total += t.total; conn += t.conn;
            if (t.conv !== null) convs.push(t.conv);
          }
          obCalled += called; obTotal += total; connTotal += conn;
          obSuccess[p.code] = called > 0 ? Math.round((conn / called) * 1000) / 10 : null;
          // Ky nam: binh quan cac ty le thang (ty le luu san theo thang).
          convRate[p.code] = convs.length ? Math.round((convs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / convs.length) * 10) / 10 : null;
        }
        const obCoverage = obTotal > 0 ? Math.round((obCalled / obTotal) * 1000) / 10 : null;
        const obSuccessTotal = obCalled > 0 ? Math.round((connTotal / obCalled) * 1000) / 10 : null;
        // Binh quan so lan goi / thue bao (IPCC), tach 4 chuong trinh
        let hasIpcc = false, totalCalls = 0, totalIpccTb = 0;
        const ipccAgg = { CKN: { calls: 0, total: 0 }, CKD: { calls: 0, total: 0 }, BG: { calls: 0, total: 0 }, HVC: { calls: 0, total: 0 } };
        const ipccUnmatched = [];
        for (const mk of curMonths) {
          const ip = await ipccMonth(mk);
          if (ip.exists) hasIpcc = true;
          for (const u of (ip.unmatched || [])) if (!ipccUnmatched.includes(u)) ipccUnmatched.push(u);
          for (const code of ["CKN", "CKD", "BG", "HVC"]) { ipccAgg[code].calls += ip[code].calls; ipccAgg[code].total += ip[code].total; }
        }
        let avgCalls = null, avgCallsTotal = null;
        if (hasIpcc) {
          avgCalls = {};
          for (const code of ["CKN", "CKD", "BG", "HVC"]) {
            const a = ipccAgg[code];
            avgCalls[code] = a.total > 0 ? Math.round((a.calls / a.total) * 100) / 100 : null;
            totalCalls += a.calls; totalIpccTb += a.total;
          }
          avgCallsTotal = totalIpccTb > 0 ? Math.round((totalCalls / totalIpccTb) * 100) / 100 : null;
        }
        const hasData = hasOb || hasTh || hasIpcc;
        let dataNote = "";
        if (!hasData) {
          dataNote = "Chưa có nguồn dữ liệu OB — khung Dashboard đã sẵn sàng, anh import dữ liệu ở mục Quản lý dữ liệu đầu vào → Import dữ liệu TTCSKH.";
        } else {
          const missing = [];
          if (!hasOb) missing.push("dữ liệu KQTH OB chi tiết (doanh thu/thuê bao các chương trình đang bằng 0)");
          if (!hasTh) missing.push("Tổng hợp Autocall (các chỉ số tiến trình OB chưa tính được)");
          if (!hasIpcc) missing.push("IPCC (Bình quân số lần gọi chưa tính được)");
          if (missing.length) dataNote = "Đã có một phần dữ liệu; chưa có " + missing.join("; ") + ".";
          if (ipccUnmatched.length) dataNote += (dataNote ? " " : "") + "IPCC có chương trình chưa nhận diện được (" + ipccUnmatched.map((x) => "'" + x + "'").join(", ") + ") — Bình quân số lần gọi của chương trình này chưa tính được, anh kiểm tra lại tên chương trình trong file IPCC nhé!";
        }
        const yearRev = ky === "nam" ? totRev : trend.reduce((a, t) => a + t.revenue, 0);
        const yearPrevRev = ky === "nam" ? totPrevRev : 0;
        // ---- Diem tin ca nhan: tong hop theo ma_hrm (14/11) ----
        // Ten dien thoai vien lay tu bang CBCNV cua dung thang do
        // (cbcnv_MMYYYY: ma_hrm -> ho_ten); ma HRM khong co trong CBCNV thi
        // hien chinh ma HRM. Dong OB khong co ma_hrm thi khong gan cho ai.
        const cbcnvByMonth = {};
        for (const mk of curMonths) {
          const table = monthTableName("cbcnv", mk);
          const map = new Map();
          if (table && (await tableExists(table))) {
            const rows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT ma_hrm, ho_ten FROM "' + table + '" WHERE ma_hrm IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(ma_hrm) <> \'\'').all()).results || [];
            for (const r of rows) {
              const k = String(r.ma_hrm).trim().toUpperCase();
              if (!map.has(k)) map.set(k, String(r.ho_ten || "").trim() || String(r.ma_hrm).trim());
            }
          }
          cbcnvByMonth[mk] = map;
        }
        const opName = (code, mk) => {
          const k = String(code || "").trim().toUpperCase();
          return (cbcnvByMonth[mk] && cbcnvByMonth[mk].get(k)) || String(code || "").trim();
        };
        const FORMS = ["Gia hạn", "Bán gói", "Nâng gói/Nâng chu kỳ"];
        const progAgg = new Map(); // CODE|HRM -> { name, subs, revenue }
        const formAgg = new Map(); // HINH THUC|HRM -> { name, subs, revenue }
        const addAgg = (map, key, code, mk, subs, rev) => {
          const name = opName(code, mk);
          const cur = map.get(key);
          if (cur) {
            // Uu tien ten that hon ma HRM (khi thang khac co ten trong CBCNV)
            if (cur.name.toUpperCase() === String(code).trim().toUpperCase() && name.toUpperCase() !== String(code).trim().toUpperCase()) cur.name = name;
            cur.subs += subs; cur.revenue += rev;
          } else {
            map.set(key, { name, subs, revenue: rev });
          }
        };
        for (const mk of curMonths) {
          for (const p of PROGS) {
            const cfg = MOB_SRC[p.code];
            const table = monthTableName(cfg.ob, mk);
            if (!table || !(await tableExists(table))) continue;
            // 1. Theo chuong trinh: SUM(DT)/1,1 + COUNT(TB) theo ma_hrm
            const rows = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT ma_hrm, COUNT(so_thue_bao) AS subs, COALESCE(SUM("' + cfg.rev + '"), 0) AS rev FROM "' + table + '" GROUP BY ma_hrm').all()).results || [];
            for (const r of rows) {
              const code = String(r.ma_hrm == null ? "" : r.ma_hrm).trim();
              if (!code) continue;
              const rev = Math.round((Number(r.rev) || 0) / 1.1);
              addAgg(progAgg, p.code + "|" + code.toUpperCase(), code, mk, Number(r.subs) || 0, rev);
              // 2. Ban goi: toan bo OBBG tinh vao hinh thuc Ban goi
              if (p.code === "BG") addAgg(formAgg, "Bán gói|" + code.toUpperCase(), code, mk, Number(r.subs) || 0, rev);
            }
            // 2. Theo hinh thuc — chi OBCKN/OBCKD/OBHVC co doanh_thu_goi_truoc_gh
            if (p.code !== "BG") {
              // HVC: TB "Ban goi tap khong goi" da tinh vao hinh thuc Ban goi (duoi),
              // nen loai khoi Gia han/Nang goi de khong cong trung (loi lech anh bao 15/11:
              // tong theo hinh thuc cao hon tong theo CT dung bang DT cua cac TB nay).
              const hvcExcl = p.code === "HVC" ? " AND COALESCE(LOWER(TRIM(ten_chuong_trinh)), '') <> 'bán gói tập không gói'" : "";
              // Gia han: DT truoc >= DT sau HOAC DT truoc = 0
              // (15/11 anh chot: TB gia han ma DT giam — goi moi re hon goi cu —
              // van tinh vao Gia han, vi ho da gia han, chi la goi re di)
              const gh = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT ma_hrm, COUNT(so_thue_bao) AS subs, COALESCE(SUM("' + cfg.rev + '"), 0) AS rev FROM "' + table + '" WHERE (COALESCE(doanh_thu_goi_truoc_gh, 0) = 0 OR COALESCE(doanh_thu_goi_truoc_gh, 0) >= COALESCE("' + cfg.rev + '", 0))' + hvcExcl + ' GROUP BY ma_hrm').all()).results || [];
              for (const r of gh) {
                const code = String(r.ma_hrm == null ? "" : r.ma_hrm).trim();
                if (!code) continue;
                addAgg(formAgg, "Gia hạn|" + code.toUpperCase(), code, mk, Number(r.subs) || 0, Math.round((Number(r.rev) || 0) / 1.1));
              }
              // Nang goi/nang chu ky: DT truoc < DT sau VA DT truoc != 0
              // (DT truoc = 0 da tinh vao Gia han theo dinh nghia cua anh, nen
              // loai ra day de 3 hinh thuc khong cong trung nhau)
              const ng = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT ma_hrm, COUNT(so_thue_bao) AS subs, COALESCE(SUM("' + cfg.rev + '"), 0) AS rev FROM "' + table + '" WHERE COALESCE(doanh_thu_goi_truoc_gh, 0) <> 0 AND COALESCE(doanh_thu_goi_truoc_gh, 0) < COALESCE("' + cfg.rev + '", 0)' + hvcExcl + ' GROUP BY ma_hrm').all()).results || [];
              for (const r of ng) {
                const code = String(r.ma_hrm == null ? "" : r.ma_hrm).trim();
                if (!code) continue;
                addAgg(formAgg, "Nâng gói/Nâng chu kỳ|" + code.toUpperCase(), code, mk, Number(r.subs) || 0, Math.round((Number(r.rev) || 0) / 1.1));
              }
              // Ban goi (phan OBHVC): chi TB co ten_chuong_trinh "Bán gói tập không gói"
              if (p.code === "HVC") {
                const bg = (await env.DB.prepare('SELECT ma_hrm, COUNT(so_thue_bao) AS subs, COALESCE(SUM("doanh_thu_dk_gh"), 0) AS rev FROM "' + table + '" WHERE LOWER(TRIM(ten_chuong_trinh)) = \'bán gói tập không gói\' GROUP BY ma_hrm').all()).results || [];
                for (const r of bg) {
                  const code = String(r.ma_hrm == null ? "" : r.ma_hrm).trim();
                  if (!code) continue;
                  addAgg(formAgg, "Bán gói|" + code.toUpperCase(), code, mk, Number(r.subs) || 0, Math.round((Number(r.rev) || 0) / 1.1));
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
        // Gop ke hoach ca nhan: doi_tuong co the la ten hoac ma HRM
        const planByKey = new Map(); // norm(doi_tuong) -> { CODE -> {pr, ps} }
        for (const dt of [...personSet]) {
          const k = normProg(dt);
          if (!planByKey.has(k)) planByKey.set(k, {});
          for (const p of PROGS) {
            const pr = personPlan[dt + "|" + p.code + "|doanh_thu"] || 0;
            const ps = personPlan[dt + "|" + p.code + "|thue_bao"] || 0;
            if (pr || ps) planByKey.get(k)[p.code] = { pr, ps };
          }
        }
        let byProgram = [];
        const matchedPlanKeys = new Set();
        for (const [key, v] of progAgg) {
          const [code, hrm] = key.split("|");
          let plan = null;
          for (const k of [normProg(hrm), normProg(v.name)]) {
            const pk = planByKey.get(k);
            if (pk && pk[code]) { plan = pk[code]; matchedPlanKeys.add(k + "|" + code); break; }
          }
          const pr = plan?.pr || 0, ps = plan?.ps || 0;
          byProgram.push({ dien_thoai_vien: v.name, ma_hrm: hrm, chuong_trinh: code, subs: v.subs, revenue: v.revenue, plan_rev: pr, plan_subs: ps, pct_plan_rev: pct(v.revenue, pr), pct_plan_subs: pct(v.subs, ps) });
        }
        // KH da giao nhung chua co so lieu thuc hien
        for (const dt of [...personSet]) {
          for (const p of PROGS) {
            const pr = personPlan[dt + "|" + p.code + "|doanh_thu"] || 0;
            const ps = personPlan[dt + "|" + p.code + "|thue_bao"] || 0;
            if (!pr && !ps) continue;
            if (matchedPlanKeys.has(normProg(dt) + "|" + p.code)) continue;
            byProgram.push({ dien_thoai_vien: dt, ma_hrm: "", chuong_trinh: p.code, subs: 0, revenue: 0, plan_rev: pr, plan_subs: ps, pct_plan_rev: pct(0, pr), pct_plan_subs: pct(0, ps) });
          }
        }
        byProgram.sort((a, b) => (a.dien_thoai_vien || "").localeCompare(b.dien_thoai_vien || "", "vi") || a.chuong_trinh.localeCompare(b.chuong_trinh));
        let byForm = [];
        for (const [key, v] of formAgg) {
          const [form, hrm] = key.split("|");
          byForm.push({ dien_thoai_vien: v.name, ma_hrm: hrm, hinh_thuc: form, subs: v.subs, revenue: v.revenue });
        }
        byForm.sort((a, b) => (a.dien_thoai_vien || "").localeCompare(b.dien_thoai_vien || "", "vi") || FORMS.indexOf(a.hinh_thuc) - FORMS.indexOf(b.hinh_thuc));
        // Phan quyen ca nhan (14/11): admin/subadmin + lanh dao don vi (role
        // leader hoac chuc_danh "Lãnh đạo đơn vị") xem het; nhan vien khac chi
        // xem so lieu cua minh (khop ma HRM, neu tai khoan khong co ma HRM thi
        // khop theo ten). Phan tap the o tren thi moi nguoi co module deu xem.
        // (Khi lam bang chat luong cuoc goi thi ap dung loc tuong tu.)
        const isPriv = me.role === "admin" || me.role === "subadmin";
        const isTLeader = me.role === "leader" || normProg(me.chuc_danh) === "lanh dao don vi";
        let indivScope = "all";
        if (!isPriv && !isTLeader) {
          indivScope = "self";
          const myCode = String(me.ma_hrm || "").trim().toUpperCase();
          const myName = normProg(me.ho_ten || "");
          const mine = (r) => (myCode && String(r.ma_hrm || "").trim().toUpperCase() === myCode) || (myName && normProg(r.dien_thoai_vien) === myName);
          byProgram = byProgram.filter(mine);
          byForm = byForm.filter(mine);
        }

        return json({
          ok: true, ky, period_label: periodLabel, has_data: hasData,
          data_note: dataNote,
          sources: { ob: hasOb, th: hasTh, ipcc: hasIpcc },
          team: {
            programs, prev_cutoff: prevCutoff,
            total: { subs: totSubs, revenue: totRev, plan_rev: totalPlanRev, plan_subs: totalPlanSubs, pct_plan_rev: pct(totRev, totalPlanRev), rev_prev: totPrevRev, rev_yoy_pct: pct(totRev - totYoyRev, totYoyRev) },
            trend,
            year: { revenue: yearRev, prev_revenue: yearPrevRev, yoy_pct: ky === "nam" ? pct(yearRev - yearPrevRev, yearPrevRev) : null },
            progress: {
              ob_coverage: obCoverage, ob_total: obTotal, ob_called: obCalled,
              conv_rate: convRate,
              ob_success: obSuccess,
              ob_success_total: obSuccessTotal, avg_calls: avgCalls, avg_calls_total: avgCallsTotal, total_calls: totalCalls, ipcc_unmatched: ipccUnmatched,
            },
          },
          individual: { by_program: byProgram, by_form: byForm, quality: [], scope: indivScope },
          plans: { has_plan: planRows.length + personRows.length > 0, team_plans: planRows.length, person_plans: personRows.length },
        });
      }
      // Dashboard ban hang kieu dieu hanh (moi):
      // - Tu dong tong hop TAT CA loai du lieu ban hang (doanh thu / san luong), khong can chon.
      // - Loai bo cung cac giao dich co ket qua "Dang ky khong thanh cong".
      // - Ky danh gia: theo thang (ky=thang) hoac theo nam (ky=nam).
      // - PSSL = diem ban co phat sinh san luong trong ky: co phat trien moi
      //   thue bao (dong san luong TNTTTB tinh duoc) HOAC co doanh thu ban
      //   goi > 0d (dong SMCS). Dong chi xuat hien trong bang ma khong sinh
      //   ra thue bao nao va doanh thu 0d thi KHONG tinh PSSL.
      if (path === "/api/sales-overview" && method === "GET") {
        if (!hasModule(me, "salesdash")) return err("Tai khoan khong co quyen xem Dashboard ban hang", 403);
        const types = await getDataTypes(env);
        const stypes = types.filter((x) => {
          if (x.sales_kind === "san_luong") return x.sales_unit_col && x.sales_eload_col;
          return (x.sales_kind === "doanh_thu" || !x.sales_kind) && x.sales_unit_col && x.sales_eload_col && x.sales_price_col;
        });
        if (!stypes.length) return err("Chua co loai du lieu nao duoc khai bao dung cho Dashboard ban hang. Vao Quan ly du lieu dau vao > Quan ly loai du lieu de thiet lap.", 422);
        const ky = url.searchParams.get("ky") === "nam" ? "nam" : "thang";
        const nowS = saigonNow();
        let thang = String(url.searchParams.get("thang") || "").trim();
        if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(thang)) thang = nowS.slice(0, 7);
        let nam = parseInt(url.searchParams.get("nam"), 10);
        if (!nam || nam < 2000 || nam > 2100) nam = Number(nowS.slice(0, 4));
        const months = [];
        let curMonths, prevMonths, c2Months, cmp1_label, cmp2_label, trendMonths, periodLabel;
        if (ky === "nam") {
          for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) months.push((nam - 1) + "-" + String(m).padStart(2, "0"));
          for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) months.push(nam + "-" + String(m).padStart(2, "0"));
          curMonths = months.slice(12); prevMonths = months.slice(0, 12); c2Months = [];
          cmp1_label = "vs năm " + (nam - 1); cmp2_label = null;
          trendMonths = curMonths; periodLabel = "năm " + nam;
        } else {
          let y = Number(thang.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(thang.slice(5, 7));
          for (let i = 13; i >= 0; i--) { let mm = m - i, yy = y; while (mm <= 0) { mm += 12; yy--; } months.push(yy + "-" + String(mm).padStart(2, "0")); }
          const T = months[13];
          curMonths = [T]; prevMonths = [months[12]]; c2Months = [months[1]];
          cmp1_label = "vs tháng trước"; cmp2_label = "vs cùng kỳ năm trước";
          trendMonths = months.slice(2); periodLabel = "tháng " + Number(T.slice(5, 7)) + "/" + T.slice(0, 4);
        }
